2026年 10月 30日 全5頁

10月会合で日銀は政策金利を据え置き 
2％の物価安定に向け利上げ継続へ 
利上げの悪影響は今のところ限定的、12月に1.5％へ追加利上げか

経済調査部 チーフエコノミスト 神田 慶司 
  エコノミスト 秋元 虹輝

[要約] 
◼ 日本銀行（日銀）は2026年10月29、30日の金融政策決定会合で、短期金利を1.25％
程度に据え置いた。日銀の政策運営は、基調的な物価上昇率を「2％に近づける」局面
から「2％程度で安定させる」局面に移った。今後は、経済・物価・金融情勢に応じて
政策金利を引き上げ、金融緩和の度合いを調整していく方針だ。 
◼ 日銀は2024年 3月以降、金融政策の正常化を進めてきたが、企業・家計への悪影響は
これまでのところ限定的だ。企業では比較的良好な資金調達環境やネット有利子負債
比率の低下が、家計では収入の増加や借入返済額の減少に加え、住宅ローン金利上昇
の返済額への反映の遅れが、足元の負担増を抑えている。ただし、家計の借入残高は
収入対比で高水準にあり、金利高が長期化すれば、返済負担が徐々に高まる可能性が
ある。 
◼ 当社では、日銀は 2026年 12月会合で短期金利を1.5％、2027年 3月会合で 1.75％へ
引き上げた後、いったん据え置くとみている。ただし、その後も金融環境がなお緩和
的で物価上振れリスクが残れば、同年6月会合で2.0％への追加利上げに踏み切る可能
性がある。

2026年 10月会合で日銀は政策金利の据え置きを決定 
日本銀行（日銀）は2026年10月29、30日に開催された金融政策決定会合で、無担保コール
レート・オーバーナイト物（以下、短期金利）を 1.25％程度に据え置くことを決めた1。今回
は利上げを決定した翌月の会合ということもあり、これまでの利上げの影響を見極めることを
重視したとみられる。

1 日本銀行「当面の金融政策運営について」（2026年10月 30日）

### Page 2

1

2 / 5

連続利上げに慎重な姿勢は、前回会合後の総裁発言からもうかがえる。9月会合直後の記者
会見で植田和男総裁は、「大きな幅の利上げとか何回も連続して利上げするというようなこと
は、端的に申し上げれば、物価が目標を超えてものすごい上がっていくというリスクが高いか、
あるいは既に目標を超えちゃっているかというようなことに依存するんだと思います」2と述べ、
その例として2022、23年の米欧の高インフレ局面を挙げた。 
また、日銀が保有する上場投資信託（ETF）については、2026年 1月以降の売却が市場に大
きな影響を与えることなく円滑に進捗したことなどを踏まえ、2027年 1月から年間の売却ペー
スを簿価で 3,300億円程度から 8,600億円程度（市場全体の売買代金に占める年間売却額（時
価）の割合は0.05％程度から0.1％程度）に引き上げることを決めた3。

インフレ基調は2％目標と概ね整合的で、今後は定着に向けて利上げ継続へ 
9月会合の声明文で「2％に近づいている」4と評価した消費者物価の基調的な上昇率につい
ては、10月会合で公表された「経済・物価情勢の展望」（展望レポート）で「2％の『物価安定
の目標』と概ね整合的な水準」に修正された。さらに、「従来は、基調的な物価上昇率を 2％に
向けて引き上げていく過程にあり、物価の下振れリスクに相応の注意を払うことが必要であっ
たが、足もとでは局面が変化しており、今後は、基調的な物価上昇率が 2％を超えて上振れて
いくリスクが顕在化し、それがその後の経済に悪影響を及ぼすことがないよう、十分に留意す
る必要がある」と指摘した5。 
植田総裁は 9月会合直後の記者会見ですでに、基調的な物価上昇率を 2％程度で安定させて
いくことが重要になったとして、 「政策の局面が変化した」と説明していた。10月会合での文
言変更は、こうした政策局面の変化をより明確にしたものといえる。また、10月 16日の記者
会見でも、今後は基調的な物価上昇率が「これ以上上がるということは容認できない」6と述べ、
2％近辺での定着を目指す考えを改めて示した。 
展望レポートでは、引き続き物価上振れリスクへの警戒を示し、経済・物価・金融情勢に応
じて政策金利を引き上げ、金融緩和の度合いを調整していく方針を前回と同様に明記した。日
銀が物価上振れリスクを見極める上で注視しているのが、①中東情勢に伴う原油価格、②AI関
連需要、③為替相場の3つである。足元ではAI関連投資の収益性への懸念が広がり、円安も一
服しているものの、日銀はこれらの動向が国内物価にどの程度波及するかを引き続き見極めて
いくとみられる。 
展望レポートを見ると、2026年度の実質 GDP成長率は春先以降のエネルギー・原材料価格の
上昇を受けて伸び率を縮小しつつも緩やかな成長を続け、2027年度以降は所得から支出への前

2 日本銀行「総裁記者会見」（2026年9月18日、24日公表） 
3 日本銀行「ETFの売却ペースの見直しに関する決定（2026年 10月）」（2026年 10月30日） 
4 日本銀行「金融市場調節方針の変更について」（2026年9月 18日） 
5 日本銀行「経済・物価情勢の展望（2026年10月）」（基本的見解、2026年10月 30日） 
6 日本銀行「植田総裁記者会見(10月 16日) ―G20終了後の植田総裁、三村財務官 共同記者会見における総裁
発言」（2026年10月 20日）

### Page 3

1

3 / 5

向きな循環メカニズムが強まるもとで成長率を緩やかに高めていくとされた（政策委員見通し
の中央値は2026年度＋0.7％、27年度＋0.7％、28年度＋0.8％） 。 
一方、消費者物価（除く生鮮食品）の前年比は、原油価格の上昇や半導体価格等の上昇、為
替円安などを背景に見通し期間前半に 2％をはっきりと上回る水準まで伸び率を高めた後、後
半には原油高の影響の減衰により、 2％程度に向けてプラス幅を縮小する見通しだ（政策委員
見通しの中央値は 2026年度＋2.5％、2027年度＋2.6％、2028年度＋2.0％） 。夏場以降の原油
価格上昇の影響などから、2027年度が前回（7月）から 0.2％pt上方修正された。消費者物価
（除く生鮮食品・エネルギー）の見通しは 2026年度＋2.4％、2027年度＋2.7％、2028年度＋
2.3％と、前回からの修正は小幅にとどまった。なお、2027年 4月から実施される可能性のあ
る飲食料品の消費税率引き下げは、物価見通しに反映されていない。 
利上げが続く中でも企業・家計への影響はこれまでのところ限定的 
日銀は2024年3月の金融政策決定会合でマイナス金利政策の解除とイールドカーブ・コント
ロール（長短金利操作）の撤廃を決め、その後 5回の利上げや国債買入れの減額を実施するな
ど金融政策の正常化を進めてきた。 
もっとも、企業や家計の経済活動への悪影響はこれまでのところ限定的で、金融環境はなお
緩和的とみられる。まず企業部門を見ると、日銀短観の業況判断DIは高水準を維持しており、
資金繰り判断DIや金融機関の貸出態度判断DIにも目立った悪化は見られない（図表1左）。中
小企業でも顕著な悪化は見られず、利上げが進む中でも、企業の景況感や資金調達環境は総じ
て良好な状態にある。 
ただし、過去の利上げ局面との単純な比較には注意が必要だ。1990年頃までの利上げは、資
産バブル期を含む高成長・高インフレ環境の下で実施されたのに対し、 2000年代以降は低成
長・デフレないし低インフレが長く続き、企業の財務構造も大きく変化した。足元で利上げの
影響が表れにくい背景には、企業の財務体質の改善がある。 
有利子負債から現預金を控除した額の総資産に対する比率（ネット有利子負債比率）は、資
本金 1千万円以上 10億円未満の中小・中堅企業で歴史的な低水準にある（図表 1右）。大企業
では中小・中堅企業ほど低下しておらず、資本金 1千万円未満の企業では相対的に高いものの、
企業部門全体では 1970～90 年代に比べて大幅に低下している。こうした財務構造の変化によ
り、政策金利が上昇しても、企業部門全体では利払い費の増加を通じた金融引き締めの影響が
以前より表れにくくなっていると考えられる。

図表 1：日銀短観の各種 DIの長期推移（左） 、企業規模別に見たネット有利子負債比率（右）

（注）左図は全産業ベースで、破線で示した中小企業は資本金 2千万円以上 1億円未満。 「利上げ局面」は利上
げ開始から利下げ直前までの期間。右図のネット有利子負債比率は、有利子負債から現預金を控除して総資産
で除したもの。資本金 1千万円未満のデータは年度値の平均（その他は四半期値の平均） 。 
（出所）日本銀行、財務省より大和総研作成

家計部門についても、これまでのところ金利上昇の影響は限定的だ。ただし、ストック面か
ら金利上昇への耐性を見ると、借入残高対年収比率は 2000年代初めから大幅に上昇している
（図表 2左）。データが遡及可能な2002年と比べると、2026年上半期の同比率は約53％pt高い。
要因別では、借入残高の増加が同比率を約 66％pt押し上げた一方、年収の増加が約 12％pt押
し下げた。家計の借入負担はストック面で以前より重くなっており、金利上昇が長期化すれば、
その影響が徐々に顕在化する可能性がある。 
図表 2：家計の借入残高対年収比率（左） 、借入返済負担率（右）の2002年からの変化幅

（注）二人以上の勤労者世帯。左図の借入残高対年収比率は、1 世帯あたりの借入残高（「住宅・土地のための
負債」と「住宅・土地以外の負債」の合計）を年間世帯収入で除したもの。右図の借入返済負担率は 1世帯あ
たりの実収入に対する借金返済額の比率。「低所得世帯」は年間収入五分位階級のうち第Ⅰ・Ⅱ分位、「高所得
世帯」は第Ⅳ・Ⅴ分位を集計。「返済世帯比率要因」は、住宅ローン返済世帯の収入が二人以上の勤労者世帯
全体の収入に占める割合の変化による寄与を表す。 
（出所）総務省より大和総研作成

### Page 5

5 / 5

一方、フロー面から足元の資金繰りを見ると、収入に対する借入返済額の比率（借入返済負
担率）は、返済額の減少や収入の増加を背景に、2002年以降の低水準にある（図表2右）。2026
年上半期の借入返済負担率は2002年を約1.1％pt下回った。要因別では、住宅ローン返済世帯
の収入シェア（住宅ローン返済世帯の収入が二人以上の勤労者世帯全体の収入に占める割合）
の上昇が負担率を押し上げた一方、返済額の減少と収入の増加が押し下げた。所得階級別に見
ると、足元では低所得世帯（年収下位 4割）の返済負担率が相対的に上昇しているものの、低
所得世帯、高所得世帯（年収上位4割）ともに2002年の水準を下回る。このため、金利上昇が
家計の資金繰りを大きく圧迫する状況には至っていない。 
ただし、足元の返済負担の低さは今後も続くとは限らない。変動金利型住宅ローンでは、毎
月の返済額の急増を抑える「5 年ルール」や「125％ルール」が広く採用されている7。こうし
た仕組みは、金利上昇の影響が返済額に反映される時期を遅らせ、短期的な負担増を和らげる
半面、負担の顕在化を将来に先送りする。借入残高が高水準にあることも踏まえると、金利高
が長期化するほど家計の返済負担が高まる可能性には留意が必要だ。

中立金利を探りながら、日銀は段階的な利上げを継続へ 
当社では、日銀は次回 2026年 12月の金融政策決定会合で短期金利を1.5％に、2027年 3月
会合で 1.75％に引き上げるとみている。その後は経済・物価・金融情勢への影響を見極めるた
め、いったん政策金利を据え置くと予想する。ただし、1.75％への引き上げ後も金融環境がな
お緩和的で、物価上振れリスクが残る場合には、同年 6月会合で 2.0％への追加利上げに踏み
切る可能性がある。 
日銀は2026年3月に公表したレポート8で、6つのモデルによる自然利子率の推計レンジを▲
0.9％程度～＋0.5％程度と示した。2％の物価上昇率を想定すると、名目の中立金利は 1.1～
2.5％程度で、1.75％はそのレンジのほぼ中央に位置する。だが、中立金利を正確に推計する
ことは困難で、その推計レンジには大きな幅がある9。そのため日銀は、各種データでこれまで
の利上げの影響や物価の基調などを確認しながら、金融緩和の度合いを徐々に調整していくと
みられる。

7 5年ルールは、変動金利型住宅ローンの金利が変動しても、毎月の返済額を5年間据え置く仕組み。125％ル
ールは、返済額の見直し時に、新たな返済額の上限を従来の 125％とする仕組み。いずれも法令で一律に定め
られたものではなく、適用の有無は金融機関や商品によって異なる。 
8 日本銀行「自然利子率の動向と金融緩和度合いの評価」（「日銀レビュー」、2026-J-5、2026年3月） 
9 2026年6月会合後の記者会見で内田眞一副総裁は、中立金利の推計値には「相当なばらつき」があるとした
上で、「短期金利を上げていく過程で、それを含めた金融環境の変化ということを点検して、中立金利の水準
を探りながら、金融緩和の度合いを調整していく」と述べた（日本銀行「総裁記者会見（内田副総裁代理）」
（2026年6月16日、17日公表））。

國巨法說會前財報預估 
預估 4Q26 營運逐步墊高，仍關注未來獲利狀況

| 財報訊息   
國巨即將於 10/29(四)舉行法說會， 3Q26營收季增 11.4%至495.3億
元，符合預期，市場普遍預估毛利率介於38~40%，EPS約4.5元，後
續將關注公司對於2027年產能規劃 、 整體產業供需(同業擴廠狀況及
AI機櫃時程)以及產品價格看法。 
| 營運現況  
3Q26營收符合預期：3Q26營收季增11.4%至495.3億元，主要受惠於
AI相關應用需求，且標準品及特殊品出貨均呈現穩定成長，營收達
成率100%，符合我們先前預期，而獲利部分，隨3Q26將有新合約逐
漸發酵，推測獲利將較上季提升，毛利率和營益率我們預估兩者分
別為39.5%和28.8%，稅後EPS達5.4元。 
| 營運展望  
本季營收預估季增 5%至520億元：我們預估4Q26營收有望季增5%
至520億元 ， 成長動能來自於AI新平台陸續放亮對於元件的需求 、 日
韓大廠的外溢訂單以及產品組合轉佳，且從10月代理商提供的交期
數據觀察，村田及三星電機的MLCC交期已長達約30及52週，趨勢
持續往上，若對照疫情期間的高峰(3Q21村田高容MLCC曾達30~33
週)，顯示供需仍吃緊，而目前擴廠及設備準備時間較長，各廠僅藉
由產線優化及產線轉換提升約10%~15%，因此推測B/B ratio 也保持
在高檔，獲利部分，根據供應鏈調查，部分日韓廠有漲價動作，國
巨有望跟進漲價，因此預估毛利率將延續上升趨勢，提升 0.75個百
分點至40.3%，稅後EPS達5.75元。

### Page 1

M
Update
Macronix International Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
Pricing hike lifts margin further;
shifting from NOR toward
NAND
3Q earnings affected by capacity reallocation:
• 3Q revenue: NT$24.2bn, +27% Q/Q, +195% Y/Y, 9% below our estimate; we
cite to capacity transition from NOR to NAND.
• Gross margin: 70.5%, +6.1ppt Q/Q, +57.0ppt Y/Y, 2.7ppt above our estimate –
helped by ~NT$750mn inventory reversal, mostly associated with NOR..
• EPS: NT$5.52, +41% Q/Q, 13% below our estimate.

•  Employee compensation accruals led to a 24% Q/Q operating expense
increase.
Shifting focus to NAND: NOR revenue increased 21% Q/Q despite a 12% Q/Q
decline in bit shipments, consistent with pricing strength. Management attributed
lower NOR shipments to capacity reallocation toward NAND; NAND revenue rose
39% Q/Q, mainly from pricing.
The first phase of capacity expansion is scheduled to enter volume production in
2H27; the additional NT$15.8bn investment could contribute in 2028.

Incremental capacity will favor NAND, in particular 3D NAND-based eMMC (i.e., MLC
NAND), with no planned increase in NOR capacity. The company will gradually
withdraw from ROM product.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Macronix International Co Ltd (2337.TW)
Base case, P/B, in line with our Greater China memory coverage, due to the industry's cyclical
nature. We apply a target multiple of 2.1x to our 2027 BVPS estimate vs. its historical average
of 1.5x since 2017. We continue to believe the P/B target is justifiable, as the MLC and legacy
TLC NAND could be in greater shortage into 2H26, with under-supply easily reaching 40%. We

believe the only supplier that could fill the gap remains Macronix.
Risks to Upside
n Wider legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR up-cycle, driven by limited supply expansion and/or stronger demand
n Faster growth for Switch hardware and core titles
Risks to Downside
n Less severe legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR down-cycle, driven by faster supply expansion and/or demand weakness
n Slower growth for Switch hardware and core titles

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 7

UMC (2303.TW)
28 October 2026 Citi Research
7
UMC
Company description
United Microelectronics Corporation (UMC) is a leading global semiconductor
foundry company. It provides high-quality IC fabrication services, focusing on
logic and various specialty technologies to serve all major sectors of the
electronics industry. UMC's comprehensive IC processing technologies and
manufacturing solutions include Logic/Mixed-Signal, embedded High-
Voltage, embedded Non-Volatile-Memory, RFSOI, and BCD. Most of UMC's

12-inch and 8-inch fabs with core R&D are located in Taiwan.
Investment strategy
We rate UMC shares a Buy. Overall inventory levels in PC, smartphone, and
consumer electronics are currently healthy, and we see limited inventory
correction downside. While macro uncertainties may linger, we believe UMC’s
niche technology offerings will help it outperform its trailing-edge foundry
peers. We also expect transfer orders from TSMC and the AI-on-device trend

to drive stronger demand, and see better UTR recovery for UMC going
forward.
Valuation
Our target price for UMC of NT$125 is based on 18x 2027 /28E EPS, which is
higher than the midpoint of its 3-year forward PE. We believe our target
multiple is justified by our belief that UMC should benefit from the robust
demand in 22/28nm, TSMC's increasing order outflow opportunity, long-term
opportunity in advanced packaging and photonics and that its niche

technology offerings should help it outperform its trailing-edge foundry
peers. Our target price equates to 2027E/28E PB of 3.8x/3.6x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) a longer-than-expected
demand recovery; 2) more competition from peers in 28nm; 3) worse-than-
expected developments from the company's China investment; 4) a
significant decline in smartphone end-demand; 5) poor contribution from
Novatek's iPhone OLED business; and 6) fiercer ASP competition from

Citigroup Global Markets Inc. and/or its affiliates has a significant financial interest in relation to UMC. (For an explanation of

Analysts’ compensation is determined by Citi Research management and Citigroup’s senior management and is based upon

activities and services intended to benefit the investor clients of Citigroup Global Markets Inc. and its affiliates (the “Firm”).
Compensation is not linked to specific transactions or recommendations. Like all Firm employees, analysts receive
compensation that is impacted by overall Firm profitability which includes investment banking, sales and trading, and
principal trading revenues. One factor in equity research analyst compensation is arranging corporate access events between

institutional clients and the management teams of covered companies. Typically, company management is more likely to
participate when the analyst has a positive view of the company.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be

published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

M
Idea
VSMC could cause  high-single digit (%) dilution to 2027 GM – above many
investors’ 3-5% expectation: On Vanguard’s latest earnings call, management noted
a successful trial run of VisionPower Semiconductor Manufacturing Company, its JV
with NXP Semiconductors. It confirmed that interposers are on track for mass
production  in 1Q27. We see revenue upside from the ramp – but depreciation could
heavily pressure margins. Management previously guided that VSMC could break

even at 30kwpm, more likely in late 2028, in our view (vs. current 8-inch margin at
30-35%). VSMC  capacity is scheduled at 44kwpm, ~100kwpm 8-inch equivalent,
about one-third of current 8-inch capacity (280kwpm). On the other hand, we think
new capacity may only run at <5kwpm in 1Q27 and <20kwpm by end-2027;
depreciation reflects 44kwpm (VSMC's full capacity) for infrastructure, clean room,
and construction. We therefore project that VSMC's gross margin dilution in full-

year 2027 could be more severe than many expect.
Despite strong customer commitment, VSMC could still dilute the corporate
margin average for at least the next five years: Since management previously
guided that VSMC could break even at 30kwpm, we view its break-even point as late
2028 or 1H29. It will then take years for GM to rise from 0% to 30-40%, even if the
capacity is fully booked. We thus expect a drag on corporate average GM for at least

the next five years. The good news: VSMC demand is skewed to high-margin
businesses –  interposers, industrial, and automotive – so the margin improvement
could be smoother  vs. peers like Hua Hong.
2027 might bring double-digit wafer price hikes and AI-related revenue at 20-
30% of the mix: During its latest earnings call, UMC guided for a 10% blended
wafer ASP increase in 2027. Given larger exposure to AI server PMIC, industrial, and
automotive, we expect Vanguard to raise 8-inch wafer prices 10-15% for the full year.

Given some customer pushback to front-loaded hikes, the price hike might be split –
twice a year, each 5-10%. Also, the company would still have L TAs expiring in 2027,
so we don’t expect aggressive wafer ASP guidance for 1Q27. With new interposer
production, we believe AI-related revenue could reach 20-30% of total revenue.
Down to UW from EW: The margin impact is larger than we initially expected and
could last 5-10 years.  Our new price target implies 2.8x our 2027e BVPS.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Vanguard could still show strong revenue/margin
performance for 2H26
4Q26 should maintain strong growth: We expect Vanguard to sustain strong
performance into 4Q26, supported by resilient demand from the industrial and
automotive end markets. In addition to a favorable demand outlook, ongoing capacity
optimization initiatives, particularly the transition from thicker-wire to thinner-wire
manufacturing processes, should enhance production efficiency, improve capacity

utilization, and support higher output. These operational improvements, coupled with a
favorable product mix and steady order momentum, could further underpin revenue
growth and profitability in 4Q26. We now expect Vanguard 8-inch process margin to reach
34-36% in both 4Q26 and 1Q27.

Key questions to ask during the upcoming earnings call on November 6:
1.  Price hike into 2027: How much of the incremental cost could Vanguard pass
through  to customers? Could we see double-digit apples-to-apples price hikes into
2027 despite the expiration of some L TAs?

2.  AI-related revenue: How much could  AI related revenue grow in 2027 if we
include both AI server PMIC and interposer production?
3.  Singapore fab – margin dilution and capacity allocation: How could  margin be
affected by VSMC in both 2027 and 2028? How much would depreciation be in
2027 and 2028? How would the company allocate 12-inch capacity between
interposers, NXP, and other customers?
4.  Consumer demand: Has the company seen any slowdown in consumer demand?

And how soon could that capacity be filled up by other sources of demand?
5.  Further capacity expansion: Might the company have further plans to expand 8-
inch capacity or second Singapore fab since the utilization rate has been reaching
>90%?

Vanguard: Earnings estimate revisions
We raise our 2026 EPS forecast 11% but lower   2027 and 2028  EPS estimates 9% and
5%, respectively:  We factor in the smooth production ramp of VSMC and see revenue
upside from our previous estimates. However, we also note more significant margin
dilution from the new fab, leading to sharp cuts in margin assumptions for 2027/28.

### Page 6

M
Idea
6
Valuation  methodology
We lower our price target (base case value) to NT$135 from NT$180:  We factor in our
earnings estimate changes for 2027-28 and also lower the intermediate growth rate
assumption from 13% to 10.5% to reflect the long-term margin dilution.
We continue to derive our price target from a residual income model, which we believe
best captures the stock's long-term value. Our key assumptions other than the
intermediate growth rate are unchanged, including a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2,

equity risk premium of 6% and risk-free rate of 2%),  and a terminal growth rate of 4.0%.
We also lower our bull and bear case values from NT$260 and NT$110 to NT$195 and NT
$80, accordingly, implying 4.0x and 1.7x our 2027e BVPS.

MS Rating
▪ We expect 2026 capex to increase beyond
2025's high level given Singapore fab

expansion, leading to long-term margin
pressure.
▪ We expect PMIC wafer price hikes in the
industry in response to strong demand from
AI. Vanguard could beneﬁt from TSMC
mature node consolidation as well as
interposer production.
▪ The new Singapore fab could cause high-
single-digit margin dilution to the corporate
average in 2027.
▪ VSMC's 12-inch capacity is all fully booked;
production will start to ramp in 2027 with
depreciation pressure during the early stage.

▪ Our UW rating is mostly because of the
margin pressure.

NT$195.00
4.0x base case 2027e BVPS
Healthy semi demand: Auto semi demand is
exceptionally strong; inventory is digested
quickly, and DDI for smartphones and large

panels shows robust growth. We forecast a
>30% revenue CAGR in 2025-28.
Competition from peers eases. We assume:
1) global semi sales growth of >20% and
foundry industry growth of >15% in 2026; 2)
better-than-expected demand for 8"
capacity; and 3) power demand for 5G
tracking ahead of schedule.
NT$135.00
2.8x base case 2027e BVPS
Semi growth slowdown: Demand has
started to pick up for LDDIC and PMIC,
while auto semis inventory digestion is
reaching an end. We forecast a 25% revenue

CAGR in 2025-28. Vanguard starts to
reverse pricing discounts to customers in
view of stronger demand and some rush
orders in the short run. We assume: 1) global
semi sales show growth in 2026; and 2) the
foundry industry shows single-digit growth
in 2026.
NT$80.00
1.7x base case 2027e BVPS
Semi growth decelerates: Auto semi demand
weakens markedly; power semi inventory
levels remain elevated. Smartphone DDI
shows a worse-than-expected decline.
Intensiﬁed competition and new Chinese

capacity dampen the overall pricing
environment. We forecast <10% revenue
CAGR in 2025-28. We assume: 1) overall
semi industry continues to decelerate in
2026 and foundry growth is stagnant; and 2)
demand for 8" capacity is weaker than
expected owing to faster product migration.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

3481 群創光電(INNOLUX)

報告日期： 2026/10/27 研究員：曾智楷 (Kevin Tseng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 2Q26 營收 637 億，毛利率 14.6% ，營利率 2.6% ，EPS 0.57
元。受惠於非顯示器比重持續提升 1H26 EPS 達 0.77 元。  
 公司今年處分三座廠房，累計獲利約為 200 億元，目前資產處
分已告一段落 ， 暫無進一步出售廠房計畫 ， 後續將配合FOPLP
投資調整既有廠區資源。  
 因上半年提前拉貨以及零組件漲價影響，消費型顯示器需求動
能相對保守；半導體事業部分， Chip-first 製程已進入量產，
每月出貨量約 4,000 萬片，而 RDL-first 與 TGV （玻璃基板）
製程部分，目前正與客戶緊密合作推進，預期玻璃基板封裝產
品將於 2028 年推出。

一、 2Q26 毛利率達 14.6% ，EPS 0.57 元 
群創 2Q26 營收 637 億，QoQ -4.6% ，YoY +13.1% ， 而獲利方面，
2Q26 營業毛利 92.7 億， QoQ -3.6% ，YoY +96.8% ，毛利率
14.6% ，QoQ +0.1ppts ，YoY +6.2ppts ，營業淨利 16.3 億，營利
率 2.6% ，2Q26 業外淨利 16.3 億，稅後淨利 46.5 億，單季稅後
EPS 0.57 元。而就上半年來看， 1H26 消費型顯示器佔整體營收
45% ，平均毛利率為 6%至 10% ；商用顯示器佔 13% ，平均毛利
率 16% 至 20%； 非顯示器領域佔42%， 平均毛利率11% 至 15% ，
受惠於商用、非顯示器比重提升， 1H26 EPS 達 0.77 元。

二、資產處分告一段落，後續聚焦 FOPLP 產能  
為充實營運資金、活化公司旗下資產，群創今年處分三座廠房， 3
月將南科模組廠以 8.8 億元售予南茂 (處分利益 6.59 億元 )，以及
將南科二廠以 63.25 億元售予矽品 (獲利約 58 億元 )。公司 4 月再
度宣布，將南科五廠房及附屬設施以總價 148.5 億元售予日月光
投控，預計處分利益高達 133 億元。累計今年群創認列的售廠總
獲利約為 197.59 億元。公司表示，目前資產處分已告一段落，暫
無進一步大規模出售廠房計畫 ， 後續將配合FOPLP 投資調整既有
廠區資源。

三. 消費性面板短期保守，持續朝高階封裝領域布局  
公司公告 9 月營收 199.95 億， MoM +6.9% ，YoY +0.7% ，3Q26
營收 575.8 億， QoQ -9.4% ，YoY -0.4% ，今年累計營收 1879.3
億， YoY +10.6% 。展望後市，在面板業務方面，受記憶體等零組
投資評等：區間操作 
目標價位：42.8~54.6 元 
     1.45x-1.85x27BVPS 
參考價位：46.75 元 
     1.58 X 2027 BVPS 
     2026/10/22 收盤

件漲價及筆電客戶提前於上半年拉貨影響，對消費性顯示器短期
動能相對保守，惟 50 吋以下 TV、高階電競與 OLED 顯示器，以
及商用顯示器支撐營運表現。而半導體事業部分，公司表示，目
前 Chip-first 製程已進入量產，每月出貨量突破 4,000 萬片，預計
今年與 2027 年將維持雙位數年增率 ， 至於RDL-first 與 TGV 製程
部分，由於 TGV 玻璃基板具備優異的散熱效率、低翹曲以及高密
度互連優勢，將瞄準 AI 與 HPC 晶片需求，目前正與全球策略夥
伴及客戶緊密合作推進 ， 預期玻璃基板封裝產品將於2028 年前推
出。預 估2026 全年營收 2451 億元 ，YoY +8.1% ， 毛利率14.2% ，
營利率 1.9%， 稅後淨利805 億 ， 全年稅後EPS 1.01 元。預 估2027
全年營收 2659 億元，YoY +8.5% ，毛利率 14.3% ，營利率 3.1% ，
稅後淨利 892 億，全年稅後 EPS 1.12 元，每股淨值 29.51 元。

四. 投資建議為 42.8~54.6 元區間操作  
公司持續投入半導體 FOPLP 領域，聚焦轉型、資產活化題材，但
考量到 4Q26 至明年上半年面板事業展望保守 ， 半導體事業部分，
後續仍須持續觀察客戶認證、量產進度及營運貢獻狀況。目前股
價交易於 2027 BVPS 約 1.64 倍 PBR 水準，以公司過往 PBR 介
於 0.4x~2.5x 區間水準，給予區間操作投資建議，目標價為
42.8~54.6 元(1.45x~1.85 x 2027 BVPS) 。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
群創光電 (INNOLUX) 為全球第三大面板廠，主要產品為大尺寸 (大
於 9 吋)與中小尺寸 (9 吋以下 )與延伸技術之薄膜電晶體液晶顯示
器(TFT-LCD) 相關產品，大尺寸產品應用於液晶電視、公共看板、
桌上型監視器、筆記型電腦與工業控制等；中小尺寸則為平板電
腦、可攜式影音播放器、車載、航空船舶、智能家居及手機、穿
戴裝置等應用，也可選擇性搭配各類型觸控面板；另針對特殊使
用需求，也提供應用於醫療、軍用、教育與電子紙應用之產品。
集團總部位於苗栗縣竹南鎮，台灣生產基地位於竹南、南科、台
南、高雄等地，另有海外生產基地則包含中國深圳、中國寧波、
中國南京、中國南海、荷蘭等地。 2025 年公司營收占比大宗消費
品(Commodity) 55% ；非大宗消費品 (Non-commodity) 16% ；非
顯示器領域群 (Non-display) 29% 。公司 NB 客戶為 DELL，液晶電
視客戶包括 Panasonic 、Sharp 、Sony、Toshiba 、Samsung 、TCL
等；手機及平板電腦客戶為 Samsung 、Amazon 、Apple 及華為；
車用面板客戶包括 BMW、福 特、General Motors 、 特斯拉等車廠。
國內主要競爭廠商為友達光電、瀚宇彩晶，國外廠商則包括

2. 群創連售三廠房，預估獲利近 200 億元 
為充實營運資金並挹注未來發展動能，群創近期積極活化旗下資
產。繼 3 月先後將南科模組廠以 8.8 億元售予南茂 (處分利益 6.59
億元 )，以及將南科二廠以 63.25 億元售予矽品 (獲利約 58 億元 )
後，群 創 於15 日再度宣布 ， 將南科五廠房及附屬設施以總價148.5
億元售予日月光投控，預計處分利益高達 133 億元。累計今年以
來，預估公司可望認列售廠總獲利達 197.59 億元。

4 
二.產業趨勢概況  
1. AI 需求推動，FOPLP 與玻璃基板市場 2030 年將突破 80 億美元 
根據  Counterpoint Research 報告，受惠於 AI 與高效能運算
(HPC) 強勁需求 ， 全球扇出型面板級封裝(FOPLP) 與玻璃基板市場
規模，預計將從 2024 年的 6.5 億美元飆升至 2030 年的 81 億美
元以上，其中 AI 與 HPC 應用將佔 FOPLP 總營收的 45.6% 。相較
於傳統有機基板，玻璃基板具備高互連密度、尺寸穩定與抗翹曲
等優勢，能支援次世代小晶片 (Chiplet) 架構與大型 AI 處理器，吸
引台積電、 Intel 、三星電機、日月光及力成等大廠積極擴展產能
與研發。區域佈局上，預計 2030 年台灣、日本與中國大陸將囊括
全球 84.8% 的面板級封裝產能。儘管目前仍面臨面板尺寸標準化
與玻璃穿孔 (TGV) 製程等量產挑戰，但在產業鏈合作推動下，整體
市場前景依舊十分樂觀。

圖三、 2024-2030 年各地區 FOPLP 面板產能預估

資料來源： Counterpoint

2. 零組件成本壓力與需求衰退交織，10 月面板價格全面持平 
根據 TrendForce 預估，進入第 4 季後，受上游零組件成本上漲壓
力遽增及終端採購需求衰退雙重影響， 10 月 TV、MNT 與 NB 三
大面板價格預估將全面持平。 TV 面板部分，雖有原廠調控產能並
喊漲，但主要品牌第 4 季採購需求季減約 5%，短期難接受漲價；
而 MNT 面板需求持續減弱，惟成本壓力使面板廠無讓步空間，買
賣雙方對持平具共識，後續將視 TV 面板能否成功漲價而定；另
外， NB 面板則因前六大品牌第 4 季採購需求恐季減近 30% ，出
貨動能嚴重萎縮，在成本高漲且降價無助爭取訂單的情況下，雙
方亦達成維持價格穩定的共識。

營收 575.8 億， QoQ -9.4% ，YoY -0.4% ，今年累計營收 1879.3
億， YoY +10.6% 。展望後市，在面板業務方面，受記憶體等零組
件漲價及筆電客戶提前於上半年拉貨影響，對消費性顯示器短期
動能相對保守，惟 50 吋以下 TV、高階電競與 OLED 顯示器，以
及商用顯示器支撐營運表現。而半導體事業部分，公司表示，目
前 Chip-first 製程已進入量產，每月出貨量突破 4,000 萬片，預計
今年與 2027 年將維持雙位數年增率 ， 至於RDL-first 與 TGV 製程
部分，由於 TGV 玻璃基板具備優異的散熱效率、低翹曲以及高密
度互連優勢，將瞄準 AI 與 HPC 晶片需求，目前正與全球策略夥
伴及客戶緊密合作推進 ， 預期玻璃基板封裝產品將於2028 年前推
出。預 估2026 全年營收 2451 億元 ，YoY +8.1% ， 毛利率14.2% ，
營利率 1.9%， 稅後淨利805 億 ， 全年稅後EPS 1.01 元。預 估2027

◼ 結 論 與 建 議 
基 於 以 下 理 由 ， 研究部 維持 力成 買 進 評 等  
(1) 記 憶 體 、 高 階 邏 輯 封 測 需 求 強 勁 ， 營 收 及 獲 利 創 歷 年 單 季 新 高 ， 目 前封
裝 / 測 試 稼 動 率 皆 超 過 90% ， 加 上 產 品 調 價 與 產 品 組 合 改 善 ， 4 Q26 營 收 、
毛 利 率 仍 有 成 長 空 間  
(2) 目 前 稼 動 率 接 近 滿 載 ， DRA M 需 求 持 續 大 於 供 給 ， NA ND 受 惠 企 業 及
SSD 需 求 增 加 ， 邏 輯 業 務 亦 維 持 雙 位 數 成 長 。 且 隨 載 板 等 材 料 成 本 上 升 ，
力 成 依 不 同 產 品 向 客 戶 反 映 成 本 ， 漲 價 幅 度 介 於 個 位 數 至 雙 位 數 ， 有 助 於
維 持 毛 利 率 改 善 趨 勢  
(3) 目 前 包 含 Flip Chip BGA 等 先 進 封 裝 相 關 業 務 已 占 集 團 營 收 30% 以 上 ，
FOPLP 客 戶 及 產 品 種 類 逐 漸 明 確 ， 設 備 取 得 情 況 亦 較 先 前 改 善 。 隨 客 戶 驗
證 完 成 及 量 產 規 模 擴 大 ， 預 期 先 進 封 裝 將 成 為 202 7 年 後 主 要 成 長 動 能  
(4) 與 AMD 、 Broad com 等 大 客 戶 推 進 先 進 封 裝 合 作 ， 布 局 FOPL P 、 CPO
等 技 術 ， 帶 動 2027 年 後 營 收 結 構 轉 型 機 會 。

+28.8% /+20 .4% ， 毛 利 率  22.6% /25.6% ， 營 益 率  16.2% /18.7% ， 稅 後 淨

◼ 重點摘要  
公 司 簡 介  
力成 (6239) 為全球 前 五 大 半 導 體 封 測 廠 ， 在 記 憶 體 領 域 為 全 球 最 大 封 測 廠 ，
記憶體品牌金士頓、東芝為股東之一。 2012 年 入 主 超 豐 (2441) 之 後 ， 切 入
邏輯 IC 封 測 領 域 。 主 要 產 品 為 DRAM 、 Flash 、 以 及 邏 輯 IC 封 測 。 目 前 美
光為力成的最大客戶，其次為東芝 Toshiba 、 金 士 頓 。 主 要 競 爭 對 手 為 ： 日
月 光 集 團 、 京 元 電 、 頎 邦 、 南 茂 等 全 球 前 十 大 台 灣 封 測 廠 。 3Q2 6 服 務 別 營
收比重為 Packaging 67 %、 SiP/Module 9%、 Testin g 2 4%。 產 品 別 營 收 比
重為 DRAM 20 %、 NAND 2 8% 、 SiP/Module 9%、 Log ic 43% 。

3.44 元。 3Q 26 營收 、 獲利 皆 創 歷 史 新 高 ， 主 因 記 憶 體 及 邏 輯 封 測 需 求 同
步 增 加 ， 稼 動 率 提 升 ， 加 上 封 裝 報 價 調 整 及 高 附 加 價 值 產 品 比 重 提 高 ， 推
升 整 體 毛 利 率 。 產品 方面 ， DRA M 訂 單 能 見 度 維 持 高 檔 ， NA ND 受惠 AI
伺 服 器 帶 動 企 業 級 SSD 需 求，邏 輯 產 品 出 貨 亦 呈 現 季 增 及 年 增 雙 位 數 成 長。

4Q26 維 持 成 長 ， 2027 年 FOP LP 進 入 量 產  
4Q26  DRA M 需 求 持 續 大 於 供 給 ， 封 測 訂 單 能 見 度 維 持 高 檔 ； NA ND 則受
惠 AI 伺 服 器 及 企 業 級 S SD 需 求 增 加 。 由 於 目 前 封 裝 與 測 試 稼 動 率 均 超 過
90% ， 預 期 4Q26 在 客 戶 需 求 增 加 下 ， 產 能 利 用 率 有 望 進 一 步 提 升 。 在價
格 方面 ， 近期 因 Substra te 等 原 材 料 成 本 上 漲 ， 力 成 已 依 不 同 產 品 及 成 本
變 化 向 客 戶 反 映 價 格 ， 漲 幅 約 介 於 個 位 數 至 雙 位 數 ， 且 客 戶 已 接 受 漲價 。
隨 漲 價 效 益 持 續 反 映 ， 以 及 產 能 逐 步 轉 向 高 附 加 價 值 產 品 ， 有 助 維 持 毛 利
率 改 善 趨 勢 。 高 階 邏 輯 方 面 ，Flip Chip BGA 於 3Q26 導入 7 5 × 7 8 m m大尺
寸 封 裝 ， 4Q26 進 一 步 導 入 7 8 × 8 8 m m規 格 ， 整 合 SoC 與 D RA M 等 元 件 ，
持 續 擴 大 AI ／ HPC 產 品 布 局 。  
2027 年 成 長 動 能 由 既 有 記 憶 體 封 測 延 伸 至 高 階 邏 輯 及 先 進 封 裝 ， 營 收 結 構
逐 步 改 善 。  記 憶 體 方 面 ， 力成 透 過 設 備 汰 換 、 既 有 產 能 優 化 及 高 階 產 品 導
入 提 升 產 出 效 率 。 邏 輯 方 面 ， AI ／ HPC 及 高 階 測 試 需 求 增 加 ， 超 豐 與 Tera 
Probe ／ Ter aPower 持 續 擴 充 相 關 產 能 ， 以 配 合 客 戶 新 產 品 需 求 。 先 進 封
裝 方 面 ， FOPL P 持 續 進 行 客 戶 驗 證 ， 朝 2027 年 量 產 目 標 推 進 ， 設 備 取 得
情況 亦 有 所 改 善 ，2027 ～ 2028 年 擴 產 計 畫 維 持 原 定 進 度 。 隨 新 產 品 陸 續 導
入 ， 以 及 先 進 封 裝 業 務 規 模 擴 大 ， 預 期 可 進 一 步 降 低 集 團 對 傳 統 記 憶 體 封
測 景 氣 循 環 的 依 賴 。

高階 封裝 營收 超過 集 團 營 收 30%  
力 成 持 續 推 進 Fan -ou t PLP 、 2 .5D ／ 3D 異 質 整 合 、 CPO 及 高 階 Flip Ch ip 
BGA 等 先 進 封 裝 技 術 ， 目 前 包 含 相 關 高 階 封 裝 業 務 的 營 收 占 比 已 超 過 集 團
營收 30% 。 FOPLP 採用 5 1 0 × 5 1 5 m m面 板 規 格 ， 主 要 針 對 A I Ch ip 及大型
運 算 模 組 等 應 用 ， 持 續 開 發 5 倍 Reticle S ize 以 下 封 裝 產 品 ， 並 投 入 超 大
尺 寸 封 裝 技 術 研 發 。 公司與 A MD、 Br oad com 等 國 際 大 客 戶 推 進 先 進 封 裝
合 作 ， A I Ch ip 、 Optical Engine 及 CPO 等 產 品 驗 證 持 續 進 行 ， 量 產 計 畫
維 持 不 變 ， 將 提升 力成 長期 成 長 動 能 及 評價 。

+28.8% /+20 .4% ， 毛 利 率  22.6% /25.6% ， 營 益 率  16.2% /18.7% ， 稅 後 淨

報價續漲

永豐觀點 
封測報價繼續溫和上揚，有利毛利率表現。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持力成買進投資建議：力成 3Q26 獲利略優預期，展望 4Q26 營收和毛
利率皆季增，FoPLP 維持 2027 年年中以前可以出貨的預期，2027 年邏輯產品線
為主要營收動能，預估 2026 年 EPS 13.86 元，2027 年 EPS 22.62 元，維持買進
建議，目標價 340 元(15X 2027 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
力成企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

力成為全球第五大封測廠， 以發展記憶體積體電路之封測業務為策略， 原以記憶體
測試業務起家 ，2000 年購入力晶後段設備後開始提供客戶封裝加測試一貫化服務 ， 
2012 年入股超豐電子(2441 TT) 後成為其大股東，並提供邏輯 IC 封裝業務。目前
持有超豐股權約 43%。主要客戶為鎧俠、SK Hynix(Solidigm)、美光、金士頓、南
亞科等。3Q26 業務比重為封裝 67%、測試 24%、SiP/Module 9%；產品比重為
DRAM 20%、Flash 28%、SiP/Module 9%、Logic 43%。AI 相關營收目前主要
來自 LPDDR、eSSD 等相關產品。 
3Q26 財報略優預期：力成 3Q26 EPS 3.55 元，略優預期的 3.48 元，毛利率優於
預期，主因產品組合轉佳、封測報價調漲，目前公司封測UTR 維持滿載，且4Q26
仍有調漲報價空間。 
4Q26 營收毛利率維持雙增：公司展望 4Q26 營收續增，三大產品線都將繼續調漲
報價，毛利率因為封測報價繼續調漲將提升，整體展望方向符合預期，獲利預估因
毛 利 率 優 於 預 期 而 上 修 ， 預 估4Q26 營 收 為275.54 億元 (+8.8%QoQ ，
+28.7%YoY)，毛利率 25.7% ，營業利益率 19.6% ， 稅 後 淨 利35.56 億元
(+36.2%QoQ，+90.8%YoY)，稅後 EPS 4.81 元。

FoPLP 進度維持，2027 年邏輯產品線為主要動能：公司主要營收來自 DRAM 和
NAND 封測，並耕耘高階 FCBGA 封裝、FoPLP 等技術已久，2027 年將逐步開花
結果 ， 目前公司在大尺寸晶片封裝已有拿到相關訂單 ， 客戶涵蓋AI 應用晶片業者 、
CSP 等，FoPLP 進度維持 1Q27~2Q27 放量，同樣屬邏輯產品線。公司對 2027
年記憶體市場仍持樂觀態度，表示韓廠產能沒開這麼快， 中國業者的產能則優先供
需中國本土需求仍不夠用，故供需狀況仍看不到反轉跡象。整體研究部認為 2027
年公司動能以邏輯產品為主，記憶體成長幅度相對溫和，預估 2026 年營收為
973.19 億元(+29.9%YoY)，毛利率 23.0%，營業利益率 16.6%，稅後淨利 102.31
億元 (+84.8%YoY)，稅後 EPS 13.86 元， 2027 年 營 收 為1,222.37 億元
(+25.6%YoY)，毛利率 26.3%，營業利益率 20.3%，稅後淨利 167.11 億元
(+63.3%YoY)， 稅後EPS 22.62 元 ， 維持買進建議 ， 目標價340 元(15X 2027 EPS)。

程曦資訊深耕客服委外市場 超過 30 年，具備長期公部門合作關
係、高客戶續約率及跨產業客服營運經驗，建立穩定營收基礎與市
場競爭優勢。隨 AI 技術逐步導入既有客服流程，有望降低人力成
本並提升營運效率，加上 AI 客服平台及軟體訂閱服務持續發展，
預期將帶動產品組合與 獲利結構改善。此外，公司取得 ISO/IEC 
42001 人工智慧管理系統認證，有助於提升高規格企業客戶的信
任度，進一步拓展民營企業市場。然而考量 AI 新事業仍處於發展
初期，短期研發費用投入較高，實際獲利貢獻仍需時間顯現，綜合
以上，研究部預估 程曦資訊 2027 年 營 收 為 13.99 億元，
Y+9.24%， 毛利率25.55%，Y+1.15ppts， 營業利益率15.22%，
Y+1.39ppts， 稅後純益1.93 億元 ，Y+16.34%，EPS 為 5.86 元，
參考同業本益比區間約 10~18 倍，給予 15 倍本益比評價，目標
價為 88 元，投資建議為買進。 
 程曦資訊(6898.TT)公司簡介 
程曦資訊(6898.TT)成立於 1993 年 8 月，於 2022 年 5 月登錄興櫃
戰略新板，同年 9 月轉至興櫃一般板，並於 2026 年 10 月 23 日正
式掛牌上櫃交易 。 公司為台灣專業企業流程委外(BPO)及客服營運服
務商，深耕客服市場逾 30 年，以「系統、流程、人力」一站式整合
服務為核心，結合 AI 技術與營運管理經驗，提供企業及政府機關完
整的客服委外解決方案。客戶結構涵蓋政府機關、國營事業、金融
保險、電子商務及國際品牌等，具備大型政府專案執行經驗與長期
客戶合作基礎。營運布 局方面，公司於台北、桃園、台中、台南、
高雄及屏東設有據點，並透過子公司程高資訊、點鑽整合行銷及印
尼程曦，分別拓展中南部客服市場、會員數據行銷及東南亞多語系
客服業務，持續擴大服務範疇與市場規模。

置、維運與整體營運管理服務，範疇涵蓋人力招募調度、標準化

總處、勞動部、1999 專線） 、國營事業 （台灣高鐵、台電、
台水）及跨國企業品牌。 
1.2 客訴處理：處理高難度消費者爭議與客訴案例，協助企業
傾聽顧客需求並優化服務流程。 
1.3 主動外撥與行銷伴銷：運用進線銷售、追加銷售與交叉銷
售經驗 ， 協助客戶將客服中心由成本中心轉型為利潤中心 。 
1.4 全通路服務 ： 整合電話 、Webchat、LINE、FB Messenger、
IG 等多軌管道，提供文字談話、論壇管理與社群服務。 
1.5 技術諮詢客服：提供3C、APP 及電機產品之專業技術問題
諮詢 、 線上故障排除與報修支援 （如OPPO、Realme、Plaud 
AI 等客戶） 。

營運現況 
公部門與企業客服專案帶動成長，研發及人事費用增加壓抑獲利 
程曦資訊 1H26 營收為 6.22 億元，Y+11.28%，主要受惠公部門及
企業客戶客服委外需求持續成長，帶動常態性年約服務與任務型專
案收入增加。其中，常態性年約服務涵蓋移工服務、社會住宅及長
照等政府專案，提供穩定營收基礎；任務型專案則受惠普發現金客
服專案認列，加上企業 AI 客服及數位轉型需求增加，推升整體營運
規模。獲利方面，受惠高毛利任務型專案貢獻增加，以及人力調度

與營運效率改善，毛利率提升至 23.94%，Y+0.63ppts；然而公司

未來展望 
既有合約與任務型專案支撐營收成長，AI 研發投入壓抑獲利表現 
程曦資訊 3Q26 營收為 3.24 億元 ，Y+8.40%， 主要受惠常態性年約
服務穩定貢獻，加上國合會熊本賑災 1988 專線、世界盃足球賽期
間台灣運彩客服擴編等任務型專案挹注。展望4Q26，隨運動部 「揮
汗有禮」活動持續推進，加上政府及企業年度客服委外需求延續，
預期營收可望維持成長。獲利方面，公司持續 將 AI 智能質檢、AI 
Trainer 及 AI Agent 等工具導入客服流程，雖有助於降低人力工時
及提升服務效率，但短期研發與人事費用增加，預期仍將壓抑營業
利益率表現。綜合以上，研究部預估程曦資訊2026 年營收為 12.80

AI 客服座艙提升營運效率，軟體訂閱服務推動獲利結構改善 
展望 2027 年，程曦資訊將持續推動 AI 客服座艙與全通路服務平台
整合，透過 AI IVR 、即時知識查詢、自動化通話摘要及智能質檢等
功能，降低客服人員重複性作業與教育訓練成本。隨相關應用逐步
導入既有客服專案，預期可降低營收成長對人力擴編的依賴，改善
傳統 BPO 業務的人力成本結構；另一方面，公司規劃將客服系統與
AI 應用模組轉化為雲端租賃服務，透過月費訂閱及功能加購增加軟
體收入來源， 公司目標 2027 年 AI 相關新業務營收占比 可望 達
5%~8%。考量初期收入規模仍有限，預期2027 年獲利改善主要來
自既有業務效率提升，隨後續訂閱客戶增加，長期有望進一步帶動
毛利率成長。 
圖二、程曦資訊智能客服座艙服務流程

第一金證券投資顧問股份有限公司(First Capital Management Inc.)台北市長安東路一段 22 號 11F   TEL(02) 2563 -6262 
6 
AI 治理認證強化競爭優勢，民營企業市場拓展擴大成長空間 
程曦資訊目前營收主要來自公部門， 2025 年公部門營收占比達
76%，未來將在既有公部門業務基礎上，積極拓展金融、能源、交
通及電商等民營企業市場，擴大跨產業客戶服務規模。10M26 公司
取得 ISO/IEC 420 01 人工智慧管理系統認證，結合既有 ISO/IEC 
27001 資訊安全管理能力，可望強化AI 應用在資料保護、風險管理
及法規遵循方面的可信賴度，提高承接大型企業 客服專案的競爭
力。另一方面，公司可透過既有客服營運經驗，進一步提供AI 流程
改造 、 數據分析及顧問服務 ， 增加單一客戶的服務項目與收入貢獻。
綜合以上，研究部預估程曦資訊 2027 年營收為 13.99 億元，
Y+9.24%，毛利率 25.55%，Y+1.15ppts，營業利益率 15.22%，
Y+1.39ppts，稅後純益 1.93 億元，Y+16.34%，EPS 為 5.86 元。 
圖三、程曦資訊 2025 年客戶組成

資料來源：公司資料、第一金投顧彙整

結論與建議 
程曦資訊深耕客服委外市場超過 30 年，具備長期公部門合作關係、
高客戶續約率及跨產業客服營運經驗，建立穩定營收基礎與市場競
爭優勢。隨 AI 技術逐步導入既有客服流程，有望降低人力成本並提
升營運效率，加上 AI 客服平台及軟體訂閱服務持續發展，預期將帶
動產品組合與獲利結構改善。此外，公司取得 ISO/IEC 42001 人工
智慧管理系統認證，有助於提升高規格企業客戶的信任度，進一步

第一金證券投資顧問股份有限公司(First Capital Management Inc.)台北市長安東路一段 22 號 11F   TEL(02) 2563 -6262
7
拓展民營企業市場。然而考量 AI 新事業仍處於發展初期，短期研發
費用投入較高，實際獲利貢獻仍需時間顯現，綜合以上，研究部預
估程曦資訊 2027 年營收為 13.99 億元 ，Y+9.24%， 毛利率25.55%，
Y+1.15ppts，營業利益率 15.22%，Y+1.39ppts，稅後純益 1.93
億元，Y+16.34%，EPS 為 5.86 元，參考同業本益比區間約 10~18
倍，給予 15 倍本益比評價，目標價為 88 元，投資建議為買進。

유틸리티업종

◼ 목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적, 소규모 SMR 수주 등은 긍정적 
◼ 구체적인 미국 원전 사업개발 시작, 2H27 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
◼ 2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조

목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적 양호, 소규모 SMR 수주는 긍정적 
투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 85,000원으로 하향. 이제는 성장 스토리보다
는 실질적인 원전 수주 확대가 필요한 상황. 의미 있는 수준의 원전 수주 확
대는 2H27부터 본격화될 가능성 존재. 다만, 단기적인 원전 수주 모멘텀 부
족 등을 감안해 6개월 목표주가를 현실화했음 
1) 3Q26 연결기준 영업이익 2,612억원(+91%, 이하 YoY), 에너빌리티 부문 
영업이익 1,211억원(+179%) 등 양호한 실적 기록. 이는 에너빌리티의 가스
터빈, 원전 기자재 매출 증가 등에 기인, 2) 10월 29일 NuScale과 1개 모듈 
주기기 공급 계약을 체결했으며, 4Q26 X-energy와 4개 모듈의 일부 장납기 
품목/소재 수주가 예상되는 등 2Q26 ~ 3Q26 TerraPower와의 일부 품목 계
약에 이어 개별 건의 규모는 작지만 다수의 SMR 관련 기자재 수주가 늘어나
는 점은 긍정적. 3) 최근 폴란드 AP1000 3기의 선발주 장납기 품목 확정(폴
란드 공급업체의 대상 생산 품목 확정, 원자로 압력용기 및 증기발생기는 미
확정)되었으며, 미국 원전 프로젝트가 구체화되기 시작. 4) 향후 AP1000향 
공급 기자재 품목 확대 가능성도 존재. 이는 2H27 본격적인 원전 수주금액 
확대로 이어질 것으로 기대

미국 원전은 구체적 사업개발 시작, 2H27 이후 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
2026년 6월 DOE의 ‘원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 발표(AP1000 10기)’, 8월 
TVA의 ‘2026 IRP’에 2040년까지 최대 원전 5.4GW 승인, 9월 한국과 미국 
상무부 간의 원전 투자 8기 MOU 체결 등을 통해 미국 원전 건설 재개 정책 
및 제도 개편이 구체화되고 강화되었음. 미국 원전 시장은 4Q26 ~ 2027년 
구체적인 사업개발 단계를 거쳐(부지 선정, 원전 운영 SPC 설립, 금융계약체
결, COL 신청 등), 2H27 ~ 2028년 장납기품목 선발주, 2028 ~ 2029년 건
설 계약 단계 진입 예상. 동사는 4Q26 미국 및 국내 가스터빈, 국내 공공 해
상풍력 프로젝트용 터빈, 폴란드 AP1000 주기기 등 미국 원전 이외 분야에
서의 수주 확대 예상. 현재 Rolls-Royce 등과도 SMR 주기기 공급 관련 논의 
중이며, NuScale SMR은 2027년 PPA 체결 시, COL 및 FID 이전 SMR 모듈 
11기(4Q26 1기 계약)의 수주가 가능할 것으로 기대

2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조 
대우건설·두산에너빌리티 컨소시엄의 체코 원전 시공 계약 협상은 종료되었
지만, 2027년 7월 신규 시공사와 계약 예정. 동사는 입찰에 다시 참여할 예
정. 이번 이슈의 핵심 쟁점은 이미 체코 측과 공사비총액(Lump-sum) 계약을 
맺은 한수원이 시공사와 총액계약을 맺기를 원한 것임. 이번 이슈로 체코 두
코바니 원전 EPC 프로젝트 수익성에 대한 우려가 발생할 수 있지만, 실제 건
설 과정에서 추가 비용이 공사비 계약금액(예비비 포함)을 초과할지는 예단하
기 어려움. 하도급 관리, 공기 준수 등 시공능력의 중요성이 강조되는 상황. 
동사는 3년 전부터 체코에 시공 인력 상주하며 시공 환경 자료 확보, 초기 인
허가, 설계 업무 관여해 왔으며, 주기기 공급사로서 기자재 특성을 활용해 설
치까지 수행 가능한 경쟁력 등을 바탕으로 성공적 수주를 목표하고 있음

KOSPI 5937.66

표 3. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 
항목 공식 발표 내용 
조건부 대출 약정 규모 최대 175억 달러·US$17.5 billion 
지원 대상 최대 5개 프로젝트, 프로젝트당 AP1000 2기, 합계 10기 
프로젝트 소유 구조 Westinghouse와 해당 전력회사 또는 에너지기업이 공동 소유 
대출 접근 전 자기자본 요건 프로젝트당 Westinghouse 5억 달러, 상대 전력회사·에너지기업 5억 달러, 합계 10억 달러를 사전에 확약 
조달 방식 Westinghouse가 참여 기업과 함께 장납기품목을 고정가격으로 조달 
조달 시점 자기자본 확약 시점과 기타 조건에 따라 프로젝트별 구매를 순차적으로 진행

표 4. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업의 대상 장납기품목(LLI; Long-Lead Items) 
 품목 설명  대출과 관련된 확인 근거 
원자로압력용기 
(RPV; Reactor Pressure Vessel) 
핵연료와 노심을 수용하고 원자로 냉각재의 압력을 유지하는 
용기. 앞서 설명한 격납용기와 구분되는 원자로 주기기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
증기발생기 
(SG; Steam Generator) 
원자로에서 나온 열을 전달받아 터빈을 돌릴 증기를 만드는 대
형 열교환기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
사전제작 구조 모듈 
(PSM; Prefabricated Structural Modules) 
원전 건물의 벽체·바닥 등 구조물을 모듈로 미리 제작하는 방
식의 구성품. AP1000에는 강판과 콘크리트로 구성하는 구조 
모듈이 사용됨 
DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23. 다만, 미국 대
출자료에 개별 모듈 코드까지 열거되지는 않음 
원자로냉각재펌프 
(RCP; Reactor Coolant Pump) 
원자로 냉각재를 순환시켜 원자로에서 발생한 열을 증기발생
기로 전달하는 펌프 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
격납용기 
(CV; Containment Vessel) 
원자로압력용기와 주요 설비를 둘러싸며 방사성 물질의 외부 
방출을 억제하는 대형 강철 밀폐 구조물 
DOE EDF 담당자의 직접 발언에서 확인 — 
2026.10.20 공개 인터뷰 
자료: DOE, 대신증권 Research Center

Document 297: Cotality-Westpac NZ First Home Buyer Report, Oct 2026 (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cotality-Westpac NZ First Home Buyer Report, Oct 2026 (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 19
Status: ok

### Page 1

October 2026
First Home
Buyer Report

### Page 2

2 © 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
Cotality’s
First Home Buyer
(FHB) Report has tracked
purchasing activity for
several years,
analysing
the
types of properties bought,
the prices paid, and broader
shifts in entry
-
level demand.
The latest edition updates
these measures and extends
the analysis to examine FHB
activity across different
price brackets.
Now in its fourth edition in
partnership with Westpac,
the report also draws on the

bank’s mortgage lending
data to provide additional
insight into borrower
characteristics. That
includes the average age of
FHB borrowers, a measure
not widely available
elsewhere, as well as loan
-
to
-
value ratios across new
mortgages.
For clarity, we define a first home
buyer purchase to be where no party
involved has owned property in New
Zealand before and are using a
mortgage to make the deal.
Note:
The Westpac FHB age and LVR data
are sourced from their lending records

and is not matched to the Cotality
database for this report.
Cotality – Westpac
First Home Buyer Report

### Page 3

3
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
FHBs’ market share remains
at record high levels.
In Q3
2026, they accounted for
29.5% of property purchases
across NZ, surpassing the
previous mark of 28.3% only
set three months earlier. The
number of deals remains at
historically high levels too,
bucking the wider trend of
slowing sales volumes for
groups such as investors and
relocating owner
-
occupiers.
This strength for FHBs is
being seen right across the

country,
with market shares
generally running above their
own long
-
term averages
(since 2005). The wider
Wellington area remains a
hotspot for FHBs, accounting
for 37% of activity so far in
2026. But FHBs have also
been at least 30% of the
market in Hamilton and
Auckland, as well as Gisborne,
Rotorua, Hastings,
Palmerston North, and
Invercargill
–
South Waikato
and Waikato District too.
This high level of activity
hasn’t meant too many
compromises either;

standalone houses have
comprised 76% of all FHB
purchases so far in 2026, up
from 75% last year, and the
highest figure since 2020
(78%). To be fair, FHBs have
had to pay a little more, with a
median price so far this year
of $715,000, but still less than
nearly $719,000 in 2022.
FHBs are more active across
the value spectrum too.
For
example, in the middle 40% of
the market by value, their
share rose from 21% in 2015 to
28% in 2025, and now about

30% so far this year.
Access to low deposit
finance under the LVR rules
remains a key support for
first home buyers.
Recent
Reserve Bank data shows that
more than half of FHB loans
are done at less than 20%
deposit (or above 80% LVR),
and Westpac’s own lending
records show a recent
average LVR for FHBs of a
touch more than 80%.
The average age for FHBs on
Westpac’s books has ticked
higher lately, from around 35
late last year to about 35.5
now.
But rather than

necessity due to affordability
pressures, this is more likely
to reflect a choice from many
buyers to just wait for the
right property without fear the
market will run away again in
the meantime.
Looking ahead, although it’s
never easy to get that first
home, conditions should
remain
favourable
for FHBs.
Mortgage rates remain lower
than at many points in the
past, KiwiSaver access for at
least part of the deposit
remains a strong support, and
the banks remain willing and

able to lend at low
deposit/high LVR too.
This is clearly a ‘good
new
s
story’ which we should never
get blasé or complacent
about,
after so many previous
years (even decades) of
intense housing affordability
pressures and a declining
home ownership rate.
As we head into November’s
election, the bottom line is
that first home buyers are
starting from a strong base.
Key findings
Cotality – Westpac
First Home Buyer Report

### Page 4

4
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
It’s been another solid few months
for first home buyers with their share
of house sales remaining around
record highs across the country.
That’s a stark contrast to the
softness in the broader housing
market over the past year.
Helping to support those high levels
of first home buyers have been a
couple of big trends. The first is the
large number of properties available
for sale. That’s meant those looking

to get their foot on the property
ladder have had plenty of options. As
a result, first home buyers have often
been able to get into newer and
larger standalone homes without
having to pay significantly more.
The other factor that has boosted
first home buyer numbers has been
the relatively low level of mortgage
rates in recent years. Combined with
broadly flat house prices, that’s
meant servicing a mortgage has
been much more affordable, with the

share of disposable income being
spen
t
on debt
-
servicing costs back
around pre
-
pandemic averages. For
the average first home buyer, lower
mortgage interest rates in recent
years shaved around $560 off their
monthly mortgage payments
compared to 2023 when interest
rates were at their peak. And in
Auckland, where first homes tend to
be more expensive, the saving is
around $685 a month.
While those are encouraging trends
for first home buyers, there are some

big changes in economic conditions
occurring which are likely to have
important implications for the
housing market over the year ahead.
Most notable has been the sharp rise
in inflation in the wake of the Middle
East war, with oil prices rocketing
higher since the start of this year.
That contributed to consumer prices
rising by 3.7% in the year to
September. Relatively high core
inflation levels mean we expect
inflation will push above 4% before

the end of this year and remain high
for much of 2027.
In response to those mounting
inflation pressures, and concerns
that they will persist for some time,
the RBNZ began lifting the Official
Cash Rate (OCR) back in July. We
expect further hikes in coming
months. The resulting pressure on
borrowing costs has seen mortgage
rates push higher in recent months,
with one
-
and two
-
year fixed rates
up around 0.75 percentage points
over the past year.

Even with the recent rise in
mortgage rates, the housing market
is still a lot more accessible for first
home buyers than a few years ago.
But with growing upward pressure
on interest rates, it’s a good time for
prospective first home buyers (or
anyone with a re
-
fix coming up) to
think about the best way to structure
their borrowing. That could help
insulate them from the likely trend
higher in the OCR over the next
couple of years and could save them

a lot of money over the life of their
mortgage. There’s a relatively
modest difference between short
-
and longer
-
term fixed mortgage
rates right now, which means the
cost of taking this ‘insurance’ is not
prohibitive.
Commentary from Satish Ranchhod, Westpac NZ Senior Economist

Average age of first home buyers from Westpac loans (2018 to 2026)
Providing some offset to the lift in
borrowing costs, house price
inflation is low, with effectively no
growth in average house prices for
three years now. And with mortgage
rates pushing higher, prices are
expected to remain soft for some
time. We expect that average house
prices will fall by 1.4% over 2026,
with a rise of just 1.8% in 2027.

At the same time, the supply of
housing is continuing to lift, with
more than 40,000 new dwellings
consented over the past year.  We
expect population growth to
increase, but from low levels, which
means the demand for housing might
not be shifting sharply higher any
time soon.
That combination of conditions
means that there is still likely to be a
lot of options
f
or
those looking to get
into their first home over the coming
year.

Source: Cotality, Westpac

### Page 6

6
© 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
The national picture
Since our previous FHB Report
back in May 2026, the wider
housing market has remained
subdued and patchy
–
generally
sales volumes have been flat to
falling, while property values have
been showing a similarly soft
picture, albeit not everywhere.
Amongst the main
centres
,
Auckland and Wellington, as
examples, have been more sluggish
than say Christchurch or Dunedin.
It’s also noteworthy that the stock

of listings available for sale remains
elevated across the country.
It’s not difficult to find the reasons
why the market remains in the
doldrums.
The economic backdrop
is still uncertain, the unemployment
rate is above average, and
mortgage rates have been drifting
higher in recent months as inflation
fears remain strong. A better
balance of physical housing supply
versus our population is also
dampening prices, while the
election has been a recent reason

for caution too. It’s harder to
measure, but ‘animal spirits’ aren’t a
key factor
at the moment
either.
FHBs have gone from strength to
strength
In this purchaser
-
friendly
environment, first home buyers
have continued to take full
advantage.
The Cotality Buyer
Classification data shows that their
market share hit a new quarterly
record high of 28.3% in Q2 2026,
only to push higher still in Q3
–
reaching 29.5%. They’re also faring
well in terms of the number of deals

too, recently hovering at around
25,000 on a 12
-
month rolling basis,
back to levels last seen (for a brief
period) at the height of the post
-
COVID boom.
Clearly, lower property values
–
still down by more than 18%
nationally from the peak
–
and
improved housing affordability
(even despite some recent rises in
mortgage rates) are helping FHBs.
But those aren’t the only factors;
access to (ever
-
growing) KiwiSaver
pots for at least part of the deposit

is also a factor, alongside decent
availability of high LVR finance at
the banks.
Indeed, the 25% allowance/speed
limit to lend to owner
-
occupiers at
less than 20% equity (or >80% LVR)
is not being tested
at the moment
and recently more than half of FHB
loans have been advanced at less
than 20% deposit.
Westpac’s own
mortgage lending records show
this in a different way
–
with the
average LVR for their loans to
FHBs in recent months still
running a touch above 80%.

LVRs
tend to be higher in Auckland, but a
bit lower in Canterbury and
Wellington.
They’re also getting more for their
money
FHBs have always tended to be
willing to compromise on location
or property type to make the shift
from renting into ownership, but
lately there hasn’t been much need
to take such an approach.
Over
2026 to date (Q3), standalone
houses have comprised 76% of all
FHB purchases, up from 75% last
year, and well above 2023’s
cyclical trough of less than 70%.

For comparison, standalone houses
have accounted for around 72% of
deals across all
buyers
year to date.
N
ational
FHB activity

### Page 8

8
      © 2026 CoreLogic NZ Limited t/as Cotality. Proprietary
This shows that FHBs are getting 
the most for their money in a 
quieter market. Admittedly, the 
median price they’ve paid so far 
this year has risen to $715,000, up 
from $701,000 in 2025 
 and also  
higher than the 2023 ($695,000) 
and 2024 figures ($692,000). But 
that latest price is still around 
$4,000 lower than in 2022, yet 
FHBs are still getting ‘more house’.
Another important point to make is 
that FHBs don’t always enter at the 
bottom of the housing ladder and 
work their way up 
 – 
many 
 actually 
enter  
well above the lowest tiers of 
the market. Indeed, the FHB 
median price paid of $715,000 this 
year is below the all
 -
 buyer figure of 
$785,000, but well above the all
 -
buyer lower quartile (bottom 25%) 
of $595,000. 
This point can be made another 
way too, with Cotality figures 
showing a rising share of activity 
for FHBs right across the price 
spectrum. Perhaps most notably, 
FHBs have accounted for 30% of 
purchases in the middle 40% price 
bracket of transactions so far this 
year, up from a 28% share in 2025, 
25% in 2020, and only 21% in 2015. 
They’ve even accounted for 15% of 
activity so far this year in the top 
30% of deals split by price.
FHBs’ average age has edged 
higher again, but probably just due 
to a lack of pressure
Meanwhile, at face value, 
Westpac’s data showing that the 
average age of 
 a 
FHB has recently 
edged back up to around 2024 
levels of 35.5 again, after a trough 
of 35 late last year, could seem at 
odds with the other, more 
encouraging trends. But rather than 
being out of necessity (
 e.g.  
due to 
affordability pressures), this could 
reflect the abundance of listings 
and subdued house prices, 
meaning that 
 some/many FHBs 
might just be choosing to wait a 
bit longer to get their ideal 
property, without fear they’ll be 
left behind by a rising market.

A solid outlook
Overall, it’s never easy to buy that 
first home and FHBs certainly 
continue to face challenges. But 
their presence has remained strong 
in recent months and, although 
nothing lasts forever, it’s not hard 
to imagine that FHBs stay a solid 
presence into 2027 as well. This is 
a ‘good 
 news 
 story
 ’ which we 
should never get 
 blasé 
 or 
complacent about, after so many 
previous years (decades) of intense 
housing affordability pressures and 
a declining home ownership rate.
Purchase price paid

The main centres
Looking at the data across
Auckland, Hamilton, Tauranga,
wider Wellington (i.e. combined
City, Lower Hutt, Upper Hutt,
Porirua), Christchurch, and
Dunedin, the national trends can
also be seen at the more granular
level
–
indeed, all of these areas
have had FHB market shares lately
that are above their own averages.
The wider Wellington area remains
a standout for FHBs, where their

share so far this year has been a
touch above 37%, well
in excess of
their long
-
term average in that
market of 30%. FHBs have also had
a market share in 2026 above 30%
in Hamilton and Auckland. The
figures have been a little lower in
Christchurch and Dunedin (both
around 28%), as well as Tauranga
(circa 25%), but in each of those
markets the figures have still been
4
-
8%
-
points above their own
averages.
Looking at the different property
types, standalone houses still

dominate in most areas, accounting
for 85
-
90% of FHB purchases in
Dunedin, Tauranga, and Hamilton
–
broadly in line with the
long term
norms in those areas. The share for
houses amongst FHB purchases is
always lower in Christchurch,
Wellington, and Auckland, but it’s
perhaps the former that stands out
most at present
–
with only 66% of
FHB purchases so far in 2026 in
Christchurch being standalone
houses, versus the historical
average of 75%. This no doubt

reflects the recent townhouse
construction upswing.
In each main
centre
, the median
FHB price is lower than the figure
for all buyers, but higher than the
lower quartile for all buyers
–
in
other words, FHBs don’t always
enter at the bottom. In terms of the
prices themselves, Auckland is the
main
centre
where FHBs pay the
most, at a median of $880,000 so
far in 2026, which is $110,000 less
than the all
-
buyer median, but
$110,000 more than the all

-
buyer
lower quartile. Median FHB prices
paid are $795,500 in Tauranga so
far in 2026, followed by Wellington
at $736,250, Hamilton $715,000,
and lower still in Christchurch and
Dunedin.

The main urban areas
Digging deeper into the next 12 key
centres
around New Zealand, the
story of relative strength for FHBs’
market share has remained in place
so far in 2026. Palmerston North
stands out with 33% of
transactions in the year to date
going to FHBs (6%
-
points above its
average), although Rotorua,
Gisborne, Hastings, and Invercargill
have also had FHB shares of 30%
or more this year.  Relative to their

own averages, Napier and Hastings
have been strongest, with figures
9%
-
points above normal.
But even in areas where FHBs have
traditionally found conditions
harder or have simply been less
interested, there’s still been relative
strength this year. For example,
Whangarei’s share going to FHBs
has been 25% in 2026 (versus
average since 2005 of 19%), Kapiti
Coast at 21% versus 17%, and
Queenstown
-
Lakes at 19% versus
15%.
Standalone houses tend to be the

dominant type of property amongst
the existing dwelling stock in many
provincial areas, and therefore also
account for a high share of FHB
purchases. So far this year, houses
have been at least 90% of FHB
purchases in Invercargill,
Palmerston North, Whanganui,
Rotorua, and Gisborne. It’s been
80
-
89% in Nelson, Kapiti Coast,
Hastings, Whangarei, Napier, and
New Plymouth. Queenstown is
lower at just 64%, but that’s likely
to reflect a wider range of property

types amongst the existing stock of
dwellings, as well as affordability
pressures which are probably
pushing FHBs towards lower price
brackets (and hence smaller
dwellings).
Indeed, in terms of median prices
paid by FHBs so far this year, the
highest figure (amongst the main
urban areas) has been in
Queenstown, at $1,275,000. Kapiti
Coast comes in at $700,000, with
Whangarei, Rotorua, Napier,
Hastings, New Plymouth, and
Nelson all at least $600,000. By

contrast, Invercargill ($480,000)
and Whanganui ($460,000) are at
the lower end.

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  硬體‧美國

Flex 
(FLEX.O/ FLEX US)

預期資料中心電源需求為獲利加速成長之主要動能 
重要訊息 
Flex FY2Q27 財報與 FY3Q27 財測均優於市場共識 ，公司上修 FY2027 資料
中心年增率自 70%至 80%，收盤股價上漲約 12%。 
評論及分析  
FY2Q27 資料中心電源需求升溫， EPS 年增 49%至 1.18 美元並優於共識。
Flex FY2Q27 (2026 年 7–9 月)營收年增 25%、季增 7%至 84.9 億美元，主
要來自資料中心電源 需求延續 ；受惠於電源產品營利率 年增 5.5ppt s 至約
16.0%、帶動產品組合轉佳， Flex FY2Q27  EPS 年增 49%、季增 18%至
1.18 美元，大致符合凱基原預估、優於市場共識 12%。 
FY3Q27 資料中心成長性可望優於 FY2Q27，預估 EPS 年增 52%至 1.32 美
元。Flex 已於 10 月完成對 EPC Power 的收購，該公司可望貢獻單季營收約
2 億美元，並有助於 800V 架構及固態變壓器( SST)的技術能力。FY 3Q27 隨
Hyperscalers 與 Neo clouds 等客戶需求延續，凱基預估 Flex 營收年增
33%、季增 11%至 93.9 億美元，其中資料中心業務之營收年增率預期達
115%，其餘產品線則約 10%上下；受惠營利率較佳的資料中心業務貢獻提
升，FY3Q27  EPS 預估年增 52%、季增 11%至 1.32 美元。 
凱基預估 Flex FY2027/FY2028  EPS 為 5.24/8.80 美元。基於電源產品接單
成長性優於預期以及併購 EPC Power 也將挹注營收，Flex 上修 FY2027 資料
中心營收年增率 自 70%至 80%，FY2028 則維持年增 80%之展望，隱含
FY2027/FY2028 資料中心營收分別上修 約 6% 、營收占比預期達
33%/45%。基於 Flex 上修展望，凱基 將 Flex FY2027/ FY2028 EPS 自原預估
之 4.96/7.96 美元上修至 5.24/8.80 美元，分別年增 59%/68%。 
投資建議  
凱基維持「增加持股」評等，目標價上修至 176 美元 (20x FY2028F EPS) 。 
投資風險  
關稅戰惡化導致客戶下單意願降低； CPI 業務營運成長態勢不如預期。  
增加持股‧維持

### Page 2

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

Flex 資料中心營收占比提升至 30%。Flex 三大產品線為 ITS (包含網通與智慧
生活，FY 2Q27 營收占比 39%；營利率 5.5%)、RMS ( 包含工業、汽車與醫
療，FY1Q27 營收占比 31%；營利率 6.4%)、CPI ( 資料中心業務，FY1Q27
營收占比 30%；營利率 10.7%)。各項業務之應用主要包含:  (1) 工業產品涵
蓋資本設備、工業裝置、再生能源、電源與電網邊緣等解決方案； (2) 車用
產品主要提供下一代移動方案、自駕、連網化 、電動化與智慧科技 ；(3) 健
康解決方案包含醫療裝置、醫療設備與藥物輸送系統； (4) 智慧生活包含消
費性電子、家電、消費性包裝、掃地機器人、微型移動裝置及音訊產品； (5) 
網通領域涵蓋通訊基礎設施與企業網路傳輸； (6) 資料中心業務 包含電源、
散熱、系統整合等產品與服務。   
Flex 目前的發展策略是結合 EMS 與產品的服務策略以提升接單能力，公司
表示自身是全球唯一具有提供從資料中心晶片到電網能力的供應商，除了可
提供嵌入式電源 (embedded power) 產品、管理伺服器 GPU 的電源，亦可
滿足客戶對資料中心內所有設施的電力與散熱需求，提供完整的客製化服
務，帶動公司資料中心營收占比自前一季之 28%提升至 FY2Q27 之 30%。

圖 1: Flex CPI部門為近幾季主要成長動能，營收占比逐步提升至30%

Flex FY2027/ FY2028 資料中心營收與獲利成長性可望優於產業平均 。Flex
計劃將 CPI 部門分拆成一家新的上市公司 Axiom，維持在 CY1Q27 完成分拆
的進度不變， Flex 自身將專注於醫療保健、機器人、自動化倉儲和網通等產
業，兩家公司可更聚焦 於彼此的業務並提升資金運用效率。公司 提及自身在
資料中心電源布局相對完整，且產品價格亦相對具有競爭優勢下，使公司在
電源產品接單成長性優於產業平均； 隨包含 Google 在內的 Hyperscalers 、
Colocations 及 Neo-clouds 等客戶對嵌入式電源與 系統級解決方案的需求升
溫，公司在此次會議將 FY2027 資料中心營收年增率自 70%上修至 80%、
FY2028 則維持年增 80%之展望，皆較 FY2026 資料中心業務營收年增 38%
至 66 億美元加速成長 ，並隱含 FY2027/FY2028 資料中心營收分別上 修約
6%、營收占比預期達 33%/45%。

### Page 3

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  3

獲利性部分， 受惠於電源產品營利率較佳、 FY2Q27 年增 5.5ppts 至約
16.0%，優於資料中心業務的平均營利率約 11%以及公司整體平均營利率
約 7%，公司上修 FY2027 資料中心業務之營利率約 1ppt 至 11.6%。凱基
預估隨資料中心營收貢獻提升， FY2027/FY2028 營 利占比分別達
49%/65%，為公司主要獲利動能。  
至於資料中心以外之 其餘應用，預期營收年增率 維持約 10%上下之水準。
目前歐洲工業需求略 有回溫，營收年增率可望維持約 10%上下；汽車產品
線則因目前全球汽車銷售量已逐步回升至過往 9,000 萬輛的正常水準，
FY2027 /FY2028 產品線成長性預期落在 5%以內；醫療設備則因客戶先前在
Covid -19 時期堆積的庫存水位過高，目前庫存去化雖逐步落底，但 需求端仍
相對平緩；網通領域則預期隨庫存去化力道放緩， FY2027 產品線營收將逐
步回溫，預期年增率 約 10%；消費性電子則因記憶體漲價、品牌廠將成本
轉嫁給消費者的影響， FY202 7 營收預期年減約 5-10%。 
Flex 評等維持「增加持股」，目標價上修至 176 美元。基於 Flex 上修資料
中心之營收與營 利率展望，凱基將 Flex FY2027/FY2028 EPS 自原預估之
4.96/7.96 美元上修至 5.24/8.80 美元，分別年增 59%/68%；相較市場共識
FY202 7/FY2028  EPS 預估為 4.48/6.82 美元，股價交易於約 12/14 倍之
FY2028 凱基與市場共識 EPS 預估。 
Flex 過往五年本益比多落於 5-20 倍之間，考量 (1) 公司在資料中心領域掌握
從晶片、液冷散熱、BBU 到電網的供應能力 ，布局相對多數同業更全面 ；(2) 
公司持續提升在 CSP 廠供應鏈之接單量，有助 FY2027 /FY2028 營收與獲利
加速成長； (3) 基於資料中心產品線營利率可望逐步提升至 10-15%、顯著
優於公司過往產品之 5%水準，且在獲利占比逐步提升至 50%以上的帶動
下，有助公司評價向散熱與電源同業靠攏。綜合上述 原因，評估 Flex 本益比
可望回升至區間 上緣之 20 倍上下浮動 ；基於 Flex 當前評價偏低 ，故維持
「增加持股」之投資評等，目標價隨 EPS 預估上修而調整至 176 美元 (20x 
FY202 8F EPS)。

### Page 7

美國 Flex

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 7

項目 定義 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用
事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下 
購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源 
再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例 
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物 
總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
環保支出 環保支出總金額 - 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處理、衛生、清理支出 
員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減
或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數 
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100 
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作 
每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數 
股東治理分數 衡量公司用以反收購工具的有效性 
公司治理分數 衡量公司對最佳治理原則的承諾和有效性 
產品責任分數 衡量公司生產優質產品和服務的能力，且產品是否將客戶的健康、安全、整合性和數據隱私進行綜合考量 
社區關係分數 衡量公司對成為優良公民、保護公眾健康和尊重商業道德的承諾 
勞動力分數 衡量公司在員工工作滿意度、健康、工作場所的安全、多樣性、平等以及員工發展機會方面的成效 
資源使用指標 衡量公司在原物料、能源或水的使用效率，以及是否通過改進供應鏈來尋求更俱生態效率的解決方案

### Page 1

#rbnz #newzealand
First Impressions: RBNZ Monetary Policy Review October 2026
By Kelly Eckhold & Westpac New Zealand Economics
12:30 October 28 2026 Share
The RBNZ left the OCR unchanged at 2.75% (in contrast to market expectation for a hike,
including ourselves).
The MPC has stuck to its strategy of pausing to assess the impact of tightening already done. There is
a clear split among the MPC members on where the risks lie although it seems clear enough that

higher interest rates are in the future the data evolves as the Committee expects. Key factors to focus
on are data on inflation expectations, the global economy (and potential downside risks there) and the

### Page 2

path of fuel prices.  We expect a 25 bp increase in the OCR in December to 3% and are reverting to
the OCR profile we had up until mid last week.  We don't think the December hike is a done deal as the
data will inform the next move. We will revert with more detail after the press conference. 
Key takeouts from the minutes below.

Media contact
Kelly Eckhold
Chief economist 
+64 9 348 9382
+64 21 786 758
Key takeouts
Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
The next policy
decision
Future policy decisions will depend
on the Committee’s judgement of the
balance of risks to medium-term
inflation.
Members discussed that information
arriving over the next six weeks
could meaningfully change the
appropriate policy stance in either
direction.
  Conditional on the central economic
outlook, members judged that the
OCR may need to increase further.
All members agreed that tighter
monetary policy is likely to be
required, but remains conditional on
the economic outlook.
  However, the future OCR path is not
pre-determined.

The medium term
direction for the
OCR
The Committee judges that gradually
removing monetary stimulus is
appropriate to return inflation to the
2 percent target mid-point while
supporting growth and employment.
Further OCR increases likely, but
are conditional on the outlook

### Page 3

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
  All members agreed that gradually
removing monetary stimulus is
consistent with achieving the
medium-term inflation target,
conditional on the outlook.
If domestic pricing pressures fail to
moderate sufficiently or broaden
beyond what costs and normal pass-
through can explain, this could
warrant further tightening.
  Conditional on the central economic
outlook, members judged that the
OCR may need to increase further.
On the other hand, subdued pricing
pressures could require holding at
future meetings.
    And if the global economy turns
sharply down, materially weakening
demand and the inflation outlook,
this could alter the desired stance,
including leading to monetary
easing.

The neutral OCR   The Committee acknowledged that
the neutral interest rate has a wide
range of plausible estimates.
    Members noted that the increase in
global long-term yields may partly
signal a higher global neutral
interest rate, due to higher inflation
expectations, war destruction and
demand for capital to support AI
investment.
    Higher global interest rates could
bring forward a potential global
downturn, and the neutral interest
rate could decline rapidly in the
event of an abrupt global
adjustment.

### Page 4

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting

The strength of
economic activity
After lacklustre growth in the June
quarter, New Zealand's economic
recovery has most likely resumed
but remains uneven.
The New Zealand economy
continues to recover, although
conditions remain uneven.
  The recovery is expected to
strengthen and broaden.
The Committee noted that GDP
growth was more than expected
through the first half of 2026.
  The Committee assessed that
growth was lacklustre in the second
quarter of 2026.
Business survey measures point to
continued expansion in economic
activity.
  The economic recovery is expected
to have resumed in the third quarter,
while remaining uneven across
sectors and regions.
Household consumption has been
weak and the aggregate saving rate
is estimated to be 3.7 percent,
above the 10-year average of 1
percent.

The global
economic
outlook
The global economy is facing
significant risks that could affect
commodity prices and demand for
exports.
Global growth has been resilient but
is increasingly fragile given
geopolitical tensions, high energy
prices, financial stresses, and
surging AI-related investment.
  Despite recent geopolitical events,
economic growth in New Zealand’s
trading partners has remained
resilient and the outlook has
improved.
The Committee assessed that there
are significant risks to the global
economic outlook.

### Page 5

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
  Although risks to the global economy
remain heightened, the Committee
expects global economic conditions
to remain favourable for New
Zealand’s export facing businesses
over the medium term.
The Committee noted that a global
downturn could occur abruptly.

The labour
market
Conditions in the labour market
should improve as the recovery
gathers pace.
Employment growth remains
subdued and higher prices will
further reduce real incomes and
purchasing power.
  Employment growth has not been
sufficient to fully absorb new
entrants into the labour market and
unemployment is elevated,
particularly in Auckland and
Wellington and for youth and the
long-term unemployed.
Employment intentions remain
weak, pointing to continued slow
recovery in the labour market.
  The labour market is expected to
slowly improve, with employment
growing and unemployment falling
as the economy recovers.
To date, wage growth has been
subdued, and medium-term inflation
expectations have remained stable.
  On balance, the Committee
assesses that spare capacity
remains in the economy, particularly
in the labour market.

### Page 6

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
The short term
inflation outlook
Inflation increased to 4.1 percent in
the June quarter because of higher
fuel prices arising from the conflict in
the Middle East.
Annual inflation is expected to
increase to 4.4 percent in the
December quarter, largely reflecting
current futures prices for fuel.
  The Committee expects inflation to
remain elevated this year before
returning to the target band by mid-
2027 and reaching the 2 percent
mid-point later next year.
Near-term inflation forecasts are
uncertain given volatility in prices for
oil and refined fuels.
    Direct effects of higher fuel prices
will drop out of the annual inflation
calculation in late 2027 if prices for
refined petroleum products stabilise
or fall.

The medium term
inflation outlook
Core inflation, expected wage
growth, and inflation expectations
remain consistent with inflation
returning to the 1 to 3 percent target
band by mid-2027 and the 2 percent
target midpoint later next year.
The Committee is focused on
returning inflation to the 2 percent
target mid-point over the medium
term, discounting temporary
disturbances to inflation and
avoiding unnecessary economic
instability.
  Longer-term inflation expectations
remain near 2 percent, while most
measures of one and two-year-
ahead inflation expectations have
fallen since May.
Impacts on shortterm pricing
behaviour appear to have been
contained and medium-term inflation
expectations remain well anchored.
  Currently, indicators of medium-term
inflation are consistent with inflation
returning to target.
But the longer inflation remains high,
the greater the risk of higher costs
feeding into inflation expectations
and price- and wage-setting
behaviour, making inflation more
persistent.

### Page 7

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting

The balance of
risks for
economic growth
and inflation
New Zealand’s economic recovery
could be stronger or weaker than
expected and price pressures could
generate more persistent inflation.
New Zealand’s recovery could be
stronger than expected but could
also lose momentum if the global
environment deteriorates or export
strength does not flow through to
jobs, incomes and household
spending.
  Consumption growth could be
subdued if employment and house
price growth remain weak, or if
precautionary behaviour persists.
There is a risk that inflation becomes
entrenched before conventional
expectations measures respond,
despite the previous two OCR
increases.
  Alternatively, consumption growth
could be stronger and absorb spare
capacity faster than currently
anticipated.
In addition, the world economy could
tip abruptly into a downturn causing
weaker inflation over the medium
term.
  All members agreed that downside
risks to activity were significant and
that the recovery could remain
uneven.
It is also possible that geopolitical
escalation could drive fuel prices
higher while weakening global
economic activity at the same time.

Key arguments
advanced by
more hawkish
MPC members
Hayley Gourley, Karen Silk,
Prasanna Gai and Anna Breman
saw upside risks to inflation relative
to the central projection.
Hayley Gourley, Paul Conway and
Karen Silk favoured increasing the
OCR by 25 basis points at this
meeting.

### Page 8

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
  These members observed that more
persistence in energy and
petrochemical prices could raise
near-term inflation risks, impact
price-setting behaviour, and lead to
more sticky inflation over the
medium term.
These members emphasised that
core inflation has been above levels
consistent with headline inflation at
the target mid-point for some time.
  Price-setting can be a front-loaded
process, meaning businesses could
raise domestic prices more than
import costs warrant.
This increases the risk of inflation
becoming entrenched in price-
setting behaviour as the conflict and
its consequences endure, including
through higher freight and shipping
costs.
  These factors increase the risk that
monetary policy needs to lean
against broader inflation pressures.
Acting now would guard against
sustained inflation pressures and
potentially reduce the risk of needing
to do more with interest rates later.

Key arguments
advanced by
more dovish MPC
members in each
meeting
Paul Conway and Carl Hansen saw
risks to inflation as balanced.
Anna Breman, Carl Hansen,
Rebecca Williams and Prasanna Gai
favoured holding the OCR at this
meeting as two recent OCR
increases allow the Committee to
take further time to assess their
effects and economic developments.
  These members shared concerns
that high inflation could become
embedded through price-setting
behaviour.
Actual inflation was lower than
expected, reflecting smaller indirect
effects of higher fuels prices, and
medium-term inflation expectations
and wage growth remain consistent
with returning inflation to the target
mid-point.

Related articles
Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
But weighed against this, they saw
activity risks as skewed to the
downside and noted that these could
weigh on inflation.
These members emphasised the
risk of a weak recovery, particularly
in household consumption and
investment.
They highlighted the potential effects
of weak house prices and
precautionary behaviour, weakening
growth in household spending.
They favoured pausing at this

meeting to observe and assess
demand and financial conditions.

Document 361: Marvell收购Lumentum：“芯片 + 光学”的首次深度闭环，“既当裁判又当运动员”与光通信的“赢家通吃”

Source PDF: all report pdf/Marvell收购Lumentum：“芯片 + 光学”的首次深度闭环，“既当裁判又当运动员”与光通信的“赢家通吃”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-31
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

Marvell 拟收购 Lumentum ，可能打通 DSP 、激光器到光模块的产业链。 Cignal AI 两位分析
师认为， AI 时代光学组件价值⼤幅提升，垂直整合有望创造更⾼利润。但 Marvell 也将⾯临
⼀个棘⼿问题：既向光模块⼚商供应芯⽚，⼜可能成为它们的竞争对⼿。
摘要：
10 ⽉ 28 ⽇，彭博报道称， Marvell 正与光学器件制造商 Lumentum 进⾏深⼊谈判，考虑通过全股
票交易收购后者。报道当时给出的 Lumentum 市值约为 830 亿美元， Marvell 约为 2210 亿美元。
这笔潜在交易引发了⼀个值得关注的问题：⼀家以定制芯⽚和⾼速互连⻅⻓的公司，为什么要
⼤举进⼊激光器和光学器件制造领域？
10 ⽉ 30 ⽇，半导体⾏业播客 Semi Doped 邀请市场研究机构 Cignal AI 的两位分析师 Andrew
Schmitt 和 Scott Wilkinson 讨论这笔交易。两⼈的判断是， Marvell 希望通过垂直整合，把 DSP 、

激光器和光学组件结合起来，形成更完整的产品体系。
如果交易完成， Marvell 可能成为少数同时覆盖 DSP 、激光器和光模块多个环节的企业。但这也
会带来新的竞争关系：它⼀边向光模块⼚商供应芯⽚，⼀边可能进⼊这些客⼾的市场。
⼀、从 DSP 到激光器、光模块： Marvell 想打通整条链
主持⼈提出， Marvell 收购 Lumentum 后，可能成为⼀家同时拥有 DSP 、激光器和光模块业务的
公司。 Andrew Schmitt 认为，最接近这⼀模式的先例是 Acacia ，但 Marvell 与 Lumentum 的组合
在业务规模和覆盖范围上可能更进⼀步。
他⾸先澄清， Marvell 并不缺激光器供应。公司⽬前主要为 ZR 相⼲模块采购可调谐激光器，与多
家供应商保持着良好关系。收购 Lumentum 的核⼼驱动⼒，是垂直整合。
Andrew 认为，在技术快速变化的市场中，企业需要同时掌握芯⽚和光学技术，才能更好地控制
产品开发和供应链。两类技术放在同⼀家公司内部，可以协同设计，做出性能更好的产品，也

能通过销售更完整的⽅案获得更多⽑利。
Lumentum ⾃⾝已经有类似的业务组合。 Andrew 举例，公司此前收购 Cloud Light ，进⼊基于硅
光技术的数据通信光模块市场。 Cloud Light ⽣产的光模块使⽤ Lumentum ⾃⼰的激光器，同时还
需要从 Marvell 、 Broadcom 等公司采购 PAM4 DSP 。
在⼀只光模块中，激光器和 DSP ⼏乎构成了最主要的价值部分，剩下的环节主要是组装。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

这意味着， Marvell 与 Lumentum 的业务本来就存在上下游联系。收购完成后， Marvell 有机会将
原本需要外部采购的关键组件纳⼊内部体系，进⼀步整合 DSP 、激光器和光学引擎。
Andrew 认为，这种模式的价值还体现在利润上。
过去，芯⽚公司的⽑利率可能达到 50% ，光学组件⼚商却只有 25% ⾄ 30% 。芯⽚公司⾃然不愿
意收购⼀家⽑利率更低的企业，担⼼拉低⾃⾝利润率。
但他表⽰，如今光学组件⾏业的⽑利率已经从约 25% 上升⾄接近 50% 。 Lumentum 在 200G
EML 、光电路交换机（ OCS ）和⾼功率激光器等产品上拥有稀缺技术，议价能⼒也明显增强。
“ 如果你能赚到更多的钱，只不过这些钱占总收⼊的⽐例低⼀些，那⼜有什么关系？ ”
Andrew 认为，投资者过去过于看重⽑利率百分⽐，忽视了企业能够创造的⽑利额。将组件整合
成更完整的产品，即使合并后的⽑利率⽐例有所变化，也可能创造更多利润。
他还提到， Marvell 六七年前可能只需约 30 亿美元就能收购 Lumentum ，如今潜在交易规模可能

达到当年的 30 倍。这是他的回忆和估算，却也反映出光学组件在 AI 时代的价值变化。
⼆、既当 “ 裁判 ” ⼜当 “ 运动员 ” ： Marvell 可能与客⼾竞争
垂直整合会让 Marvell 获得更完整的产品线，也会改变它与客⼾的关系。
Scott Wilkinson 指出， Marvell ⽬前的相⼲ DSP 被⼤量光模块⼚商采⽤。以 400G 相⼲模块为例，
除 Acacia 、 Ciena 、 Nokia 以及部分中国⼚商外，很多企业都会采购 Marvell 的 DSP 。
如果 Marvell 收购 Lumentum ，进⼀步进⼊光学引擎和模块市场，它就可能与这些原有客⼾直接
竞争。
这正是交易中⼀个微妙的地⽅： Marvell 既是产业链上游的芯⽚供应商，也可能成为下游产品的
参与者。客⼾难免会考虑，未来是否还要继续依赖⼀家与⾃⼰竞争的公司提供关键芯⽚。
主持⼈ Austin 提出了⼀个直接的问题：如果客⼾因此转向 Broadcom 、 Credo 等其他 DSP 供应

商， Marvell 是否愿意牺牲部分芯⽚市场份额，换取光模块业务？
Andrew 认为，这是⼀笔可以计算的商业账：
“ 通过垂直整合，我能够获得⽐单独销售零部件更多的价值，即使这意味着我可能会少卖⼀些零
部件。 ”
Scott 也提醒，真正需要观察的是 Marvell 如何管理收购后的业务。 Lumentum 本来就向外部客⼾
销售激光器，也经营部分光模块业务。收购完成后， Marvell 未必会把这些产能全部留给⾃⼰。
Andrew 则认为，客⼾是否流失，取决于 Marvell 能否给原有 DSP 客⼾和 Lumentum 的光学客⼾⾜
够的信⼼，让他们相信原有供货关系不会受到影响。
这也是垂直整合的代价：企业获得了更多产业链控制权，同时也可能让客⼾重新寻找供应商。
三、光通信的 “ 赢家通吃 ” ：先发优势不如百万级交付

### Page 3

除了垂直整合，两位分析师还谈到了光通信⾏业另⼀个鲜明的竞争规律：新技术标准的市场最
终往往由少数⼚商占据。
Andrew 把这⼀现象称为 “IEEE 彩票 ” 。
每当⾏业推出⼀项新标准，通常会有五六家公司同时开发产品。⼏年之后，真正占据⼤部分出
货量的往往只剩两家公司，合计拿下 80% ⾄ 90% 的市场份额。
因此，率先推出产品固然重要，但能否抓住市场形成初期的机会，才决定了企业能否成为最终
赢家。
Scott 进⼀步指出，当前 AI 光通信市场的竞争尤其看重产能。
“ 如果你第⼀个进⼊市场，却只能⽣产⼀万个，实际上没有太⼤意义。如果你第⼆个进⼊市场，
却能够⽣产⼀百万个，那么订单就会落到你⼿⾥。 ”
他以中国⼚商 Eoptolink （光迅科技）为例。这家公司在 800G 以太⽹光模块市场进⼊时间稍晚于
部分竞争对⼿，却凭借产能优势成为全球主要供应商之⼀。 Scott 认为， 1.6T 产品也正在出现类
似的竞争局⾯。
这也是 Lumentum 受到关注的原因。 Scott 提到，公司过去⼏年持续扩⼤磷化铟（ InP ）制造能

⼒，产能经历了多轮翻倍式扩张，但市场需求增⻓更快，仍然难以满⾜所有客⼾需求。
Lumentum 的优势还在于拥有⼀些竞争对⼿难以迅速复制的产品。 Andrew 特别提到 200G
EML 、 OCS 和⾼功率激光器。
他提醒，很多公司都可以宣称⾃⼰能够开发出类似产品，但真正的考验是能否从⽣产⼏百件扩
⼤到五万件、甚⾄⼀百万件。 “ 做出⼀百个和做出⼀百万个，完全是两回事。 ”
AI 时代，这种规模优势被进⼀步放⼤。 Andrew 认为，光学组件的价值也发⽣了变化：在传统电
信⽹络中，激光器主要帮助运营商增加传输容量；在 AI 数据中⼼中， 1.6T 互联可能让 AI 集群训练
速度提⾼两到三倍。
当⼀项光学技术能够显著提升整个计算集群的效率，客⼾愿意为它⽀付的价格⾃然会发⽣变
化。
四、 Nvidia 会不会加⼊竞购？
访谈最后，两位分析师讨论了 Nvidia 是否可能成为潜在买家。
Nvidia 此前已分别向 Lumentum 和 Coherent 投资 20 亿美元，并与两家公司建⽴战略合作。

Andrew 认为， Nvidia 同样有垂直整合的动⼒：此前收购 Mellanox ，就是为了把⽹络技术纳⼊⾃
⾝ GPU 系统。如果继续向光学组件和 OCS 领域延伸，也符合这⼀思路。
Andrew 还提出⼀种可能性： Marvell 与 Lumentum 的谈判消息如果是有意泄露，或许是在向其他
潜在买家释放信号，最终引发竞购战。
不过，他明确表⽰⾃⼰没有交易内幕，这只是个⼈推测。 Nvidia 此前的投资和采购合作，也不能
证明它有意参与收购。

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

1 条评论
对 Marvell 来说，这笔交易的核⼼考验最终仍是如何平衡两件事：通过整合芯⽚与光学技术获得
更⾼的整体价值，同时避免原有客⼾因为竞争关系⽽转向其他供应商。
Andrew Schmitt 的判断是，光学组件已经成为 AI 基础设施中价值更⾼的环节，芯⽚公司收购光
学企业的经济逻辑正在改变。 Scott Wilkinson 则提醒，在光通信这个容易出现 “ 赢家通吃 ” 的市
场，产能和规模决定最终份额。
Marvell 想打通从芯⽚到光学的产业链，能否同时守住客⼾、技术和市场份额，将决定这场整合
究竟能创造多⼤价值。
